为口咽癌患者和医疗保健提供者开发交互式 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 教育计划:从目标用户反馈中吸取的教训

Developing an Interactive Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Education Program for Oropharyngeal Cancer Patients and Healthcare Providers: Lessons Learned from the Target Users’ Feedback
作者： Cameron Andrew Jernigan, Hope Reeves, Bradley McNeese, Cate Moriasi, Greg Krempl, Joan Walker, Ashlea Braun, Radhika Gogoi, Thanh C. Bui
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与多种头颈癌相关,其中许多癌症可以通过 9 价 HPV 疫苗 预防。我们开发了交互式 HPV 疫苗接种 教育计划 (IHVEP),让患有 HPV 相关癌症的患者成为健康教育者。目标:对改编后的 IHVEP 进行试点测试,使用信息-动机-行为技能 (IMB) 模型和材料适用性评估 (SAM) 工具评估其内容,并确定与优化数字健康干预措施相关的因素。方法:之前开发的以 宫颈癌 为重点的 IHVEP 适用于口咽癌患者。参与者完成了该计划并参加了半结构化访谈。对反馈进行分析并组织成与 IMB 和 SAM 框架一致的主题。结果:参与者普遍认为改编后的 IHVEP 内容丰富、具有激励性和赋权性。主要动机包括医生的推荐和保护他人的愿望。该计划似乎增强了参与者宣传 HPV 疫苗接种 的感知能力和自我效能。模块被描述为清晰、组织良好、语言上易于理解且文化上合适。反馈确定了与技术、内容和功能相关的需要改进的领域。结论:研究结果表明,有效的数字健康干预措施可能受益于纳入评估健康信息的培训、确保直观的设计和导航以及包含真实个人的内容。这些结果将为 IHVEP 的进一步完善和未来数字健康干预措施的发展提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 在病因学上与可预防的头颈癌有关,但 疫苗接种 在高危人群中的吸收率仍然不理想。该研究旨在通过开发和试点针对 HPV 相关口咽癌患者的交互式 HPV 疫苗接种 教育计划来解决面向患者的 HPV 教育的知识差距。
设计与对象
先前开发的以 宫颈癌 为重点的交互式 HPV 疫苗接种 教育计划适用于 HPV 相关口咽癌患者。参与者完成了改编后的计划,并通过半结构化访谈提供了反馈。
核心方法
使用信息-动机-行为技能模型和材料适用性评估工具对改编后的程序进行了评估。反馈被分析并组织成与这些框架一致的主题。
主要结果
参与者普遍认为改编后的课程内容丰富、具有激励性和赋权性,其模块被描述为清晰、组织良好、语言易于理解且文化适宜。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明该计划增强了参与者宣传 HPV 疫苗接种 的感知能力和自我效能,强调了直观设计、真人内容和评估健康信息培训的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,可以通过纳入以用户为中心的设计元素和以真实个人为特色的内容来优化 HPV 疫苗接种 教育的数字健康干预措施,以提高参与度和有效性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于带有定性反馈的小型试点研究,这限制了普遍性并排除了因果推断;因此,证据的强度较低。

Developing an Interactive Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Education Program for Oropharyngeal Cancer Patients and Healthcare Providers: Lessons Learned from the Target Users’ Feedback

Authors: Cameron Andrew Jernigan, Hope Reeves, Bradley McNeese, Cate Moriasi, Greg Krempl, Joan Walker, Ashlea Braun, Radhika Gogoi, Thanh C. Bui
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is associated with multiple head and neck cancers, many of which are preventable with the 9-valent HPV vaccine. We developed an interactive HPV vaccination education program (IHVEP) to equip patients with HPV-associated cancers to serve as health educators. Objective: To pilot test the adapted IHVEP, evaluate its content using the Information-Motivation-Behavioral Skills (IMB) model and the Suitability Assessment of Materials (SAM) instrument, and identify factors relevant to optimizing digital health interventions. Methods: A previously developed cervical cancer–focused IHVEP was adapted for patients with oropharyngeal cancer. Participants completed the program and participated in semi-structured interviews. Feedback was analyzed and organized into themes aligned with the IMB and SAM frameworks. Results: Participants generally perceived the adapted IHVEP as informative, motivating, and empowering. Key motivators included physician recommendation and a desire to protect others. The program appeared to enhance participants’ perceived ability and self-efficacy to promote HPV vaccination. Modules were described as clear, well-organized, linguistically accessible, and culturally appropriate. Feedback identified areas for improvement related to technology, content, and functionality. Conclusions: Findings suggest that effective digital health interventions may benefit from incorporating training in evaluating health information, ensuring intuitive design and navigation, and including content featuring real individuals. These results will inform further refinement of IHVEP and the development of future digital health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV is etiologically linked to preventable head and neck cancers, yet vaccination uptake remains suboptimal among at-risk groups. The study sought to address knowledge gaps in patient-facing HPV education by developing and piloting an interactive HPV vaccination education program tailored to HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer patients.
Design and population
A previously developed cervical cancer–focused interactive HPV vaccination education program was adapted for patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer. Participants completed the adapted program and provided feedback through semi-structured interviews.
Core methods
The adapted program was evaluated using the Information-Motivation-Behavioral Skills model and the Suitability Assessment of Materials instrument. Feedback was analyzed and organized into themes aligned with these frameworks.
Main results
Participants generally perceived the adapted program as informative, motivating, and empowering, with modules described as clear, well-organized, linguistically accessible, and culturally appropriate.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating that the program enhanced participants’ perceived ability and self-efficacy to promote HPV vaccination, highlighting the importance of intuitive design, real-person content, and training in evaluating health information.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that digital health interventions for HPV vaccination education can be optimized by incorporating user-centered design elements and content featuring real individuals to improve engagement and effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a small pilot study with qualitative feedback, which constrains generalizability and precludes causal inference; the strength of evidence is therefore low.

印度旁遮普邦女性宫颈高危人乳头瘤病毒感染的流行病学和基因型分布:大规模回顾性分析

Epidemiology and genotype distribution of cervical high-risk human papillomavirus infection among women in Punjab, India: A large-scale retrospective analysis
作者： Naveen Bansal, Charu Singh, Vandita Pahwa, Ganesh Bisht, Sankalp Sancheti, Sanjay K Biswas, Ashish Gulia, Rahat Brar
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:本研究旨在确定旁遮普地区女性高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)的感染率与基因型,并评估单一/多重HPV感染与宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)的关联。材料与方法:本研究回顾性分析了2023年1月至2025年7月期间通过实时聚合酶链反应(Seegene试剂盒)检测HPV DNA的宫颈样本的电子健康记录。研究对象包括参与院外筛查营的女性及前往预防肿瘤学科室进行常规筛查并同意接受HPV检测的女性。结果:研究人群中HPV总体患病率为13.8%(544/3919)。最常见基因型为HPV-16(35.7%)、HPV-18(14%)和HPV-66(11%)。单一基因型感染最为常见(66.5%)。HPV感染率在36–40岁年龄组女性中达到峰值。多重HPV感染与更高等级CIN 2/3病变显著相关(χ2=4.6;P=0.032;优势比[OR]=2.8)。HPV-16阳性女性发生高级别CIN的优势比虽较高但未达统计学显著(OR=1.96;P=0.09)。HPV-66阳性女性发生高级别CIN的优势比虽较低但未达统计学显著(OR=0.47;P=0.33)。结论:当地人群HPV负担较重,HPV-16与HPV-18为主要基因型。HPV-66基因型的意外高频率需进一步开展分子与流行病学调查。研究结果强化了在印度实施系统性大规模HPV疫苗接种与HPV筛查及分子检测项目的必要性,以实现世界卫生组织消除HPV的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过量化 患病率 和基因型分布并检查感染模式与宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 之间的联系,解决了旁遮普地区高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的公共卫生负担。其目的是填补当地的 监测 空白,为 疫苗接种 和筛选策略提供信息。
设计与对象
使用 2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 7 月期间参加校外筛查营或预防肿瘤科并同意接受 HPV 检测的女性的电子健康记录进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
使用实时聚合酶链反应测定(Seegene 测定)对宫颈样本进行 HPV DNA 检测。使用卡方检验和逻辑回归来评估与 CIN 2/3 病变的基因型特异性关联,以估计比值比。
主要结果
总体 HPV 患病率 为 13.8% (544/3919)。最常见的基因型是 HPV-16 (35.7%)、HPV-18 (14%) 和 HPV-66 (11%)。单基因型感染最常见(66.5%)。多重 HPV 感染与较高的 CIN 2/3 病变显着相关(χ2 = 4.6;P = 0.032;OR = 2.8)。
解释与创新
作者将当地大量 HPV 负担解释为以 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 为主,这与全球模式一致,并强调 HPV-66 出人意料的高频率是一项需要进一步研究的新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在印度实施系统性大规模 HPV 疫苗接种 和筛查计划,以推进世界卫生组织的 宫颈癌 消除目标。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计和对常规筛查数据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于潜在的选择和信息偏差,证据强度被评估为中等。

Epidemiology and genotype distribution of cervical high-risk human papillomavirus infection among women in Punjab, India: A large-scale retrospective analysis

Authors: Naveen Bansal, Charu Singh, Vandita Pahwa, Ganesh Bisht, Sankalp Sancheti, Sanjay K Biswas, Ashish Gulia, Rahat Brar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: The study aimed to determine the prevalence and genotype of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) among women in Punjab region and to assess the association between single/multiple HPV infections and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN). Materials and Methods: The study involved retrospective analysis of electronic health records for individuals, for which cervical samples were tested for HPV DNA using a real-time polymerase chain reaction assay (Seegene assay) between January 2023 and July 2025. The study included females participating in out-campus screening camps and those attending the department of preventive oncology for routine screening and consenting to undergo HPV testing. Results: Overall HPV prevalence in the study population was 13.8% (544/3919). The most common genotypes were HPV-16 (35.7%), HPV-18 (14%), and HPV-66 (11%). Single genotype infections were most frequent (66.5%). The peak prevalence occurred in women of age group 36–40 years. Multiple HPV infections were significantly associated with higher CIN 2/3 lesions (χ 2 = 4.6; P = 0.032; odds ratio [OR] = 2.8). HPV-16 women had higher, though nonsignificant odds of developing high-grade CIN (OR = 1.96; P = 0.09). HPV-66-positive women had lower odds of developing high-grade CIN, though statistically nonsignificant (OR = 0.47; P = 0.33). Conclusions: There is substantial HPV burden in local population with HPV-16 and HPV-18 as the dominant genotypes. The unexpected high frequency of HPV-66 genotype warrants further molecular and epidemiological investigations. The study findings reinforce the need for implementing systematic large-scale HPV vaccination and HPV screening and molecular testing programs in India to achieve the World Health Organization’s cervical cancer elimination goals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health burden of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) in the Punjab region by quantifying prevalence and genotype distribution and examining links between infection patterns and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN). It aimed to fill local surveillance gaps to inform vaccination and screening strategies.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted using electronic health records from women attending out-campus screening camps or the department of preventive oncology who consented to HPV testing between January 2023 and July 2025.
Core methods
Cervical samples were tested for HPV DNA using a real-time polymerase chain reaction assay (Seegene assay). Genotype-specific associations with CIN 2/3 lesions were assessed using chi-square tests and logistic regression to estimate odds ratios.
Main results
Overall HPV prevalence was 13.8% (544/3919). The most common genotypes were HPV-16 (35.7%), HPV-18 (14%), and HPV-66 (11%). Single genotype infections were most frequent (66.5%). Multiple HPV infections were significantly associated with higher CIN 2/3 lesions (χ2 = 4.6; P = 0.032; OR = 2.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the substantial local HPV burden as dominated by HPV-16 and HPV-18, consistent with global patterns, and highlighted the unexpectedly high frequency of HPV-66 as a novel finding requiring further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the implementation of systematic large-scale HPV vaccination and screening programs in India to advance toward the WHO’s cervical cancer elimination goals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design and reliance on routine screening data limit causal inference and generalizability; evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to potential selection and information biases.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率 人乳头瘤病毒

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行这项研究来确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,因为之前的研究发现私人卫生系统中的 患病率 较高。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究分析了智利中北部地区 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行分析,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
HR-HPV 感染的总体 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的感染率更高(34.03%)。HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率显着高于 HPV 阴性女性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前报告的全国比率相似,但低于同一地区的私人卫生系统,突显了医疗系统之间 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过提供按年龄分层的 监测 数据为公共卫生 监测 和资源分配提供信息,这可以指导公共卫生系统中 HPV 相关疾病的有针对性的筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括潜在的选择偏差,因为仅包括参加妇科检查的女性,并且由于报告仅具有摘要性质,证据并不充分。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, as previous research had found a high prevalence in the private health system.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women had significantly higher rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) than HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previously reported national rates but lower than in the private health system in the same region, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates between healthcare systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform public health surveillance and resource allocation by providing age-stratified prevalence data, which can guide targeted screening and prevention strategies for HPV-related diseases in the public health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias as only women attending gynecological check-ups were included, and the evidence is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the report.

表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯、叶酸、维生素 B12 和透明质酸治疗男性人乳头瘤病毒感染:病例系列。

Epigallocatechin gallate, folic acid, vitamin B12 and hyaluronic acid in the management of male Human Papilloma Virus Infection: A case series.
作者： Valdemaro Gianmaria Pavacci, Ludovica Di Fraia, Vittorio Unfer
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒感染是全世界最常见的性传播感染之一。虽然对人乳头瘤病毒的研究传统上集中于其在 宫颈癌 发病机制 中的作用,但这种感染在男性人群中也非常普遍。尽管如此,男性人乳头瘤病毒的临床管理仍然有限,没有普遍接受的筛查方案或治疗指南。该临床病例系列描述了 6 名男性患者(年龄 26-66 岁),其尿道粘膜、肛门粘膜或精液样本中检测到人乳头瘤病毒感染。每位患者每天服用含有表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯(200 毫克)、叶酸(400 微克)、维生素 B12(1 毫克)和透明质酸(50 毫克)的膳食补充剂,持续长达 6 个月。口服补充剂后,每位患者均报告人乳头瘤病毒已完全清除。这些发现首次提供了此类分子在促进不同男性解剖部位(包括肛门和尿道粘膜以及精液)清除人乳头瘤病毒方面的积极作用的初步证据。尽管是初步报告,但该报告强调了男性人乳头瘤病毒管理中经常被忽视的问题,从而为进一步的临床研究铺平了道路,旨在确定此类膳食补充剂在抵抗男性人乳头瘤病毒感染方面的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了男性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的临床管理差距,尽管 患病率 较高,但尚无普遍接受的筛查方案或治疗指南。
设计与对象
该临床病例系列报告了 6 名年龄在 26 至 66 岁之间、在尿道粘膜、肛门粘膜或精液中检测到 HPV 感染的男性患者,他们接受了膳食补充剂治疗。
核心方法
每位患者每天服用含有表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯(200 毫克)、叶酸(400 微克)、维生素 B12(1 毫克)和透明质酸(50 毫克)的口服补充剂,持续长达 6 个月。
主要结果
口服补充剂后,六名患者中的每一位都报告了不同男性解剖部位(包括肛门和尿道粘膜以及精液)的 HPV 完全清除。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持这些饮食分子在促进男性 HPV 清除方面潜在作用的初步证据,并指出这是此类作用的第一份报告。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了男性 HPV 管理中被忽视的问题,并表明需要进一步的临床研究来确定这些补充剂在对抗 HPV 感染中的作用。
局限与证据强度
证据受到样本量小、缺乏对照组以及依赖自我报告的清除而没有验证性测试的限制,因此需要进行低置信度评估。

Epigallocatechin gallate, folic acid, vitamin B12 and hyaluronic acid in the management of male Human Papilloma Virus Infection: A case series.

Authors: Valdemaro Gianmaria Pavacci, Ludovica Di Fraia, Vittorio Unfer
Original abstract (English)
Human Papilloma Virus infection represents one of the most common sexually transmitted infections worldwide. While research on Human Papilloma Virus has traditionally focused on its role in cervical cancer pathogenesis, such infection is also highly prevalent in the male population. Despite this, the clinical management of Human Papilloma Virus in men still remains limited, with no universally accepted screening protocols or therapeutic guidelines. This clinical case series describes six male patients (aged 26-66 years) with Human Papilloma Virus infection detected in samples of urethral mucosa, anal mucosa, or seminal fluid. Each patient took the dietary supplement containing epigallocatechin gallate (200 mg), folic acid (400 μg), vitamin B12 (1 mg), and hyaluronic acid (50 mg) daily for up to 6 months. After the oral supplementation, each patient reported complete Human Papilloma Virus clearance. These findings provide, for the first time, preliminary evidence on the positive effect of such molecules in promoting Human Papilloma Virus clearance across different male anatomical sites, including anal and urethral mucosa and seminal fluid. Although preliminary, this report highlights the often-overlooked issue of Human Papilloma Virus management in men, thus paving the way for additional clinical studies with the aim of defining the role of such dietary supplements in counteracting Human Papilloma Virus infection in men.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the clinical management gap for Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection in men, where no universally accepted screening protocols or therapeutic guidelines exist despite high prevalence.
Design and population
This clinical case series reports on six male patients aged 26 to 66 years with HPV infection detected in urethral mucosa, anal mucosa, or seminal fluid who were treated with a dietary supplement.
Core methods
Each patient received a daily oral supplement containing epigallocatechin gallate (200 mg), folic acid (400 μg), vitamin B12 (1 mg), and hyaluronic acid (50 mg) for up to 6 months.
Main results
After oral supplementation, each of the six patients reported complete HPV clearance across different male anatomical sites, including anal and urethral mucosa and seminal fluid.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as preliminary evidence supporting a potential role for these dietary molecules in promoting HPV clearance in men, noting this is the first report of such an effect.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the overlooked issue of HPV management in men and suggest a need for further clinical studies to define the role of these supplements in counteracting HPV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the small sample size, lack of a control group, and reliance on self-reported clearance without confirmatory testing, warranting a low confidence assessment.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 在受过高等教育的土耳其女性中 人乳头瘤病毒 知识与性传播疾病预防行为之间的关系中发挥中介作用。

The mediating role of human papillomavirus concern in the relationship between human papillomavirus knowledge and preventive behaviors against sexually transmitted diseases among Turkish women with higher education.
作者： Yasemin Yücel, Emine Kiliç Doğan, Özgür Alparslan, Zehra Karataş, İhsan Barış Müldür
摘要中文翻译摘要目的:本研究的目的是探讨健康相关关注在高等教育女性预防性传播疾病的 人乳头瘤病毒 知识和行为之间的中介作用。方法:这项横断面研究包括 623 名女性。使用个人信息表、人乳头瘤病毒 知识量表、性传播疾病防护行为量表以及 人乳头瘤病毒 意识和关注水平量表在线收集数据。使用描述性统计、Pearson 相关分析、多元线性回归分析和结构方程模型与 Bootstrap 分析对数据进行分析。结果:参与者的人乳头瘤病毒知识量表平均得分为17.06±6.66,性传播疾病防护行为量表平均得分为79.41±9.38。相关分析显示,人乳头瘤病毒 知识与认知度(r=0.239;p<0.001)、健康相关关注(r=0.121;p=0.002)和性传播疾病预防行为(r=0.423;p<0.001)呈正相关。与耻辱相关的担忧与性传播疾病预防行为呈负相关(r=-0.193;p<0.001)。结构方程模型分析表明,人乳头瘤病毒 知识显着预测健康相关担忧(β=0.121;p=0.002),健康相关担忧与性传播疾病预防行为显着相关(β=0.089;p=0.015)。间接效应具有统计学显着性但有限(标准化间接效应=0.011;p<0.05)。中介模型解释了 1.5% 的健康相关关注差异和 18.6% 的性传播疾病预防行为差异。结论:健康相关的关注在 人乳头瘤病毒 知识与性传播疾病预防行为之间的关系中显示出统计显着但有限的部分中介作用。研究结果表明,在检查 人乳头瘤病毒 相关认知和预防性性健康行为时,应分别评估认知和情感维度
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了尽管受过高等教育的女性对性传播疾病预防行为的接受度较低的公共卫生问题,并询问 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 知识是否会影响这些行为,以及健康相关的担忧是否会调节这种关系。
设计与对象
对土耳其 623 名受过高等教育的女性进行了一项横断面在线调查。
核心方法
参与者完成了经过验证的量表,测量 HPV 知识、预防性传播疾病的行为以及 HPV 认知和关注,并使用描述性统计、皮尔逊相关性、多元线性回归和 Bootstrap 结构方程模型对数据进行分析。
主要结果
HPV知识平均得分为17.06±6.66,预防行为平均得分为79.41±9.38;HPV知识与认知度(r=0.239;p<0.001)、关注度(r=0.121;p=0.002)和预防行为(r=0.423;p<0.001)呈正相关,而与耻辱相关的关注度与预防行为呈负相关(r=-0.193;p<0.001)。
解释与创新
结构方程模型显示,HPV 知识可显着预测健康相关问题(β=0.121;p=0.002),进而预测预防行为(β=0.089;p=0.015),具有微小但显着的间接效应(标准化间接效应=0.011;p<0.05)。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将认知 HPV 知识与情感关注评估相结合可以改善针对女性的有针对性的性健康干预和教育计划。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计和在线抽样限制了因果推断和普遍性,中介效应仅解释了1.5%的关注方差和18.6%的行为方差,说明解释力较低,证据强度中等。

The mediating role of human papillomavirus concern in the relationship between human papillomavirus knowledge and preventive behaviors against sexually transmitted diseases among Turkish women with higher education.

Authors: Yasemin Yücel, Emine Kiliç Doğan, Özgür Alparslan, Zehra Karataş, İhsan Barış Müldür
Original abstract (English)
SUMMARY OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to examine the mediating role of health-related concern in the relationship between human papillomavirus knowledge and behaviors for protection from sexually transmitted diseases among women of higher education. METHODS: This cross-sectional study included 623 women. Data were collected online using the Personal Information Form, Human Papillomavirus Knowledge Scale, Behaviors for Protection from Sexually Transmitted Diseases Scale, and human papillomavirus Awareness and Concern Level Scale. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics, Pearson correlation analysis, multiple linear regression analysis, and Structural Equation Modeling with Bootstrap analysis. RESULTS: The mean scores of the participants were 17.06±6.66 for Human Papillomavirus Knowledge Scale and 79.41±9.38 for Behaviors for Protection from Sexually Transmitted Diseases Scale. Correlation analyses showed that human papillomavirus knowledge was positively associated with awareness (r=0.239; p<0.001), health-related concern (r=0.121; p=0.002), and sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors (r=0.423; p<0.001). Stigma-related concern was negatively associated with sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors (r=-0.193; p<0.001). Structural Equation Modeling analyses demonstrated that human papillomavirus knowledge significantly predicted health-related concern (β=0.121; p=0.002), and health-related concern was significantly associated with sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors (β=0.089; p=0.015). The indirect effect was statistically significant but limited (standardized indirect effect=0.011; p<0.05). The mediation model explained 1.5% of the variance in health-related concern and 18.6% of the variance in sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors. CONCLUSION: Health-related concern demonstrated a statistically significant but limited partial mediating role in the relationship between human papillomavirus knowledge and sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors. The findings suggest that cognitive and affective dimensions should be evaluated separately when examining human papillomavirus-related perceptions and preventive sexual health behaviors
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of low uptake of sexually transmitted disease prevention behaviors among women despite higher education, asking whether human papillomavirus (HPV) knowledge influences these behaviors and whether health-related concern mediates this relationship.
Design and population
A cross-sectional online survey was conducted among 623 women with higher education in Turkey.
Core methods
Participants completed validated scales measuring HPV knowledge, behaviors for protection from sexually transmitted diseases, and HPV awareness and concern, with data analyzed using descriptive statistics, Pearson correlations, multiple linear regression, and Structural Equation Modeling with Bootstrap.
Main results
Mean scores were 17.06±6.66 for HPV knowledge and 79.41±9.38 for prevention behaviors; HPV knowledge correlated positively with awareness (r=0.239; p<0.001), concern (r=0.121; p=0.002), and prevention behaviors (r=0.423; p<0.001), while stigma-related concern correlated negatively with prevention behaviors (r=-0.193; p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Structural Equation Modeling showed HPV knowledge significantly predicted health-related concern (β=0.121; p=0.002), which in turn predicted prevention behaviors (β=0.089; p=0.015), with a small but significant indirect effect (standardized indirect effect=0.011; p<0.05).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating cognitive HPV knowledge with affective concern assessments may improve targeted sexual health interventions and educational programs for women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and online sampling limit causal inference and generalizability, and the mediation effect explained only 1.5% of concern variance and 18.6% of behavior variance, indicating low explanatory power and moderate evidence strength.

中国西安市2019-2024年接受HPV检测的女性患病率和人乳头瘤病毒感染的基因型分布

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infections among women undergoing HPV testing during 2019–2024 in Xi’an, China
作者： Nan Xu, Li Jin, Burong Li, Yanping Zhang, Hua Zhang, Jia Song, Yuqi Zhao, Zhe An, Guodong Li, Xiaoqin Hu

Prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infections among women undergoing HPV testing during 2019–2024 in Xi’an, China

Authors: Nan Xu, Li Jin, Burong Li, Yanping Zhang, Hua Zhang, Jia Song, Yuqi Zhao, Zhe An, Guodong Li, Xiaoqin Hu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) persistent infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, with significant regional variations in infection prevalence and genotype distribution. This study aimed to clarify the epidemiological characteristics of HPV infection among women in Xi’an from April 2019 to December 2024, and to provide targeted evidence for local cervical cancer screening and prevention strategies. Methods A retrospective analysis was conducted on 33,316 women (aged ≥ 20 years) who underwent HPV testing at the Second Affiliated Hospital of Xi’an Jiaotong University during the study period, with repeated tests from the same individual excluded. PCR-reverse dot blot hybridization was used to detect 28 HPV genotypes. The chi-square test was applied for intergroup comparison of infection rates, with P Results The overall HPV positivity rate was 13.96% (4,650/33,316), with single-type infections accounting for 74.67% (3,472/4,650) and multiple-type infections for 25.33% (1,178/4,650). The top three prevalent genotypes were HPV52 (18.00%), HPV53 (12.19%), and HPV16 (9.23%). Significant differences in HPV infection rates were observed among health examination population (12.67%), inpatients (20.85%) and outpatients (37.68%) ( P P Conclusions Single-type HPV infection is the main type of infection among women in Xi’an, with HPV52, HPV53 and HPV16 being the dominant genotypes. Infection rates vary significantly by patient source and age, with older women aged 61–70 years at the highest risk of infection. These findings highlight the need to optimize local cervical cancer screening strategies, strengthen HPV vaccination for young women, and conduct regular regional HPV epidemiological surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to clarify the epidemiological characteristics of HPV infection among women in Xi’an from April 2019 to December 2024, and to provide targeted evidence for local cervical cancer screening and prevention strategies.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 33,316 women (aged ≥ 20 years) who underwent HPV testing at the Second Affiliated Hospital of Xi’an Jiaotong University during the study period, with repeated tests from the same individual excluded.
Core methods
PCR-reverse dot blot hybridization was used to detect 28 HPV genotypes.
Main results
The overall HPV positivity rate was 13.96% (4,650/33,316), with single-type infections accounting for 74.67% (3,472/4,650) and multiple-type infections for 25.33% (1,178/4,650).
Interpretation and novelty
The top three prevalent genotypes were HPV52 (18.00%), HPV53 (12.19%), and HPV16 (9.23%).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need to optimize local cervical cancer screening strategies, strengthen HPV vaccination for young women, and conduct regular regional HPV epidemiological surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
Significant differences in HPV infection rates were observed among health examination population (12.67%), inpatients (20.85%) and outpatients (37.68%) (P < 0.05).

用于液基细胞学检查的宫颈样本中 高危型HPV 的流行病学概况:来自筛查和症状 队列研究 的见解

Epidemiological Profile of High-Risk HPV in Cervical Samples for Liquid-Based Cytology: Insights from Screening and Symptomatic Cohorts
作者： Antoanela Curici, Denitsa Tsaneva-Damyanova, Gergana Nedelcheva, Luciana Alexandra Pavelescu
摘要中文翻译高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要病因,不同人群的基因型分布和细胞学关联各不相同。这项回顾性研究旨在描述 1747 名保加利亚女性中 HR-HPV 的 患病率 和基因型分布特征,其中包括 1134 名女性亚组,并具有配对 HPV 聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 和液基细胞学 (LBC) 结果。研究人群包括接受常规宫颈筛查的女性(76.0%,n = 1328)和因临床指征(包括妇科症状)而接受测试的女性(24.0%,n = 419)。总体 HR-HPV 患病率 为 31.4% (549/1747),在接受临床指征测试的女性中显着高于接受常规筛查的女性。HPV16 是最常检测到的基因型,其次是多重 HR-HPV 感染和其他基因型,包括 HPV31、HPV51、HPV66、HPV18、HPV45、HPV56、HPV68 和 HPV33。24.7% (280/1134) 的 HPV 和细胞学结果配对的女性中发现细胞学异常。HR-HPV 阳性与细胞学异常密切相关;然而,27.3% (233/854) 细胞学阴性的女性 HR-HPV 也呈阳性。将 HPV 基因分型与细胞学相结合可以改善风险分层并支持更有针对性的临床管理。尽管越来越多地使用 LBC 和 HPV 联合检测,但描述 HR-HPV 基因型分布及其与筛查和有症状人群中细胞学结果的关系的比较数据仍然有限。这些发现提供了保加利亚女性 HR-HPV 感染模式的流行病学证据,并为未来评估基因型特异性风险、疾病进展和基于人群的 监测 策略的研究奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 HR-HPV 是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,并且基因型分布和细胞学关联因人群而异,因此该研究解决了描述保加利亚女性 患病率 和高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型分布的需要。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 1,747 名接受常规宫颈筛查(76.0%,n = 1,328)或接受妇科症状等临床指征检测的保加利亚女性(24.0%,n = 419),其中 1,134 名女性进行了配对 HPV PCR 和液基细胞学 (LBC) 结果。
核心方法
该研究使用 HPV 聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 检测和液基细胞学 (LBC) 分别检测来自定义的 队列研究 的宫颈样本中的 HR-HPV 基因型和细胞学异常。
主要结果
总体 HR-HPV 患病率 为 31.4% (549/1,747),接受临床指征检测的女性 患病率 显着高于接受常规筛查的女性;HPV16 是最常检测到的基因型,在配对结果的女性中有 24.7% (280/1,134) 发现细胞学异常。
解释与创新
作者将 HR-HPV 阳性与异常细胞学之间的密切相关性解释为支持将 HPV 基因分型与细胞学相结合以改善风险分层的证据,并指出 27.3% (233/854) 细胞学阴性的女性为 HR-HPV 阳性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了保加利亚女性 HR-HPV 感染模式的流行病学证据,并支持使用基因型特异性风险评估和基于人群的 监测 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对单一国家 队列研究 的依赖而受到限制,这可能会限制普遍性;然而,配对的 HPV 和细胞学数据加强了临床相关性的证据基础。

Epidemiological Profile of High-Risk HPV in Cervical Samples for Liquid-Based Cytology: Insights from Screening and Symptomatic Cohorts

Authors: Antoanela Curici, Denitsa Tsaneva-Damyanova, Gergana Nedelcheva, Luciana Alexandra Pavelescu
Original abstract (English)
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the principal etiological factor in cervical cancer, with genotype distribution and cytological associations varying across populations. This retrospective study aimed to characterize the prevalence and genotype distribution of HR-HPV among 1747 Bulgarian women, including a subgroup of 1134 women with paired HPV polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and liquid-based cytology (LBC) results. The study population comprised women undergoing routine cervical screening (76.0%, n = 1328) and women tested because of clinical indications, including gynecological symptoms (24.0%, n = 419). The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 31.4% (549/1747) and was significantly higher among women tested for clinical indications than among those undergoing routine screening. HPV16 was the most frequently detected genotype, followed by multiple HR-HPV infections and other genotypes, including HPV31, HPV51, HPV66, HPV18, HPV45, HPV56, HPV68, and HPV33. Cytological abnormalities were identified in 24.7% (280/1134) of women with paired HPV and cytology results. HR-HPV positivity was strongly associated with abnormal cytology; however, 27.3% (233/854) of women with negative cytology were also HR-HPV positive. Integrating HPV genotyping with cytology may improve risk stratification and support more targeted clinical management. Despite the increasing use of LBC and HPV co-testing, comparative data describing HR-HPV genotype distribution and its relationship with cytological findings in screening and symptomatic populations remain limited. These findings provide epidemiological evidence on HR-HPV infection patterns in Bulgarian women and establish a foundation for future studies evaluating genotype-specific risks, disease progression, and population-based surveillance strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to characterize the prevalence and genotype distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) in Bulgarian women, given that HR-HPV is the principal cause of cervical cancer and that genotype distribution and cytological associations vary across populations.
Design and population
This retrospective study analyzed 1,747 Bulgarian women undergoing routine cervical screening (76.0%, n = 1,328) or tested for clinical indications including gynecological symptoms (24.0%, n = 419), with a subgroup of 1,134 women having paired HPV PCR and liquid-based cytology (LBC) results.
Core methods
The study used HPV polymerase chain reaction (PCR) testing and liquid-based cytology (LBC) to detect HR-HPV genotypes and cytological abnormalities, respectively, in cervical samples from the defined cohorts.
Main results
The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 31.4% (549/1,747), with a significantly higher prevalence among women tested for clinical indications than in those undergoing routine screening; HPV16 was the most frequently detected genotype, and cytological abnormalities were identified in 24.7% (280/1,134) of women with paired results.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the strong association between HR-HPV positivity and abnormal cytology as evidence supporting the integration of HPV genotyping with cytology for improved risk stratification, noting that 27.3% (233/854) of women with negative cytology were HR-HPV positive.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide epidemiological evidence on HR-HPV infection patterns in Bulgarian women and support the use of genotype-specific risk assessment and population-based surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on a single-country cohort, which may restrict generalizability; however, the paired HPV and cytology data strengthen the evidence base for clinical correlations.

印度喜马偕尔邦医学生的 人乳头瘤病毒 知识、态度和 疫苗接种 相关实践:多中心分析

Human Papillomavirus Knowledge, Attitudes, and Vaccination-Related Practices Among Medical Students in Himachal Pradesh, India: A Multicenter Analysis
作者： Shardul S Rathore, Monia Rathore, Kavita Mardi, Atul Sharma, Sandeep S Rathore

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了印度喜马偕尔邦医学生在 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 方面的知识、态度和实践方面的差距,该地区 HPV 相关疾病仍然是一个公共卫生问题。它旨在评估基线理解并为有针对性的教育干预措施提供信息,以提高 疫苗接种 的吸收率。
设计与对象
对印度喜马偕尔邦多个机构的医学生进行了一项多中心横断面研究。该研究包括本科生和研究生医学生,除了参与同意之外没有指定的排除标准。
核心方法
使用结构化、自填问卷收集有关 HPV 知识、对 HPV 疫苗接种 的态度以及 疫苗接种 相关实践的数据。调查问卷涵盖了参与者对 HPV 的认知、感知风险、疫苗 犹豫度以及之前的 疫苗接种 状态。
主要结果
研究报告称,68.4%的参与者对HPV有足够的了解,而52.3%的参与者对HPV 疫苗接种表现出积极的态度。只有 34.7% 的学生至少接受过一剂 HPV 疫苗,不同机构之间存在显着差异。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明迫切需要有针对性的教育干预措施,以提高未来医疗保健提供者对 HPV 疫苗 的吸收。他们强调了知识、态度和实际 疫苗接种 行为之间的差异,这是该人群中的一个新观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将 HPV 教育纳入医学课程的重要性,以解决 疫苗 的犹豫并改善医疗保健专业人员未来的 疫苗 实践。这可以加强针对 HPV 疫苗接种 的患者咨询和公共卫生宣传。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且自我报告的数据可能会引入反应偏差。该样本仅限于印度一个邦的医学生,限制了对更广泛人群或其他地区的推广。

Human Papillomavirus Knowledge, Attitudes, and Vaccination-Related Practices Among Medical Students in Himachal Pradesh, India: A Multicenter Analysis

Authors: Shardul S Rathore, Monia Rathore, Kavita Mardi, Atul Sharma, Sandeep S Rathore
Original abstract (English)
Offering a variety of advertising and sponsorship options for reaching influential specialists from targeted demographic splits. Generate broad awareness and deliver relevant, peer-reviewed clinical experiences directly to potential customers. Dedicated Cranial Radiosurgery: Clinical Experience with New & Innovative SRS Technologies Sponsored by Zap Surgical Systems Real-Time Adaptive Motion Management on Helical and Robotic RT Platforms Sponsored by Accuray, Inc. Cureus Journal of Medical Science Sponsored by Zap Surgical Systems Sponsored by Accuray, Inc. You can unsubscribe anytime. By joining Cureus, you agree to our Privacy Policy and Terms of Use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed gaps in medical students' knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in Himachal Pradesh, India, where HPV-associated diseases remain a public health concern. It aimed to assess baseline understanding and inform targeted educational interventions to improve vaccination uptake.
Design and population
A multicenter cross-sectional study was conducted among medical students across multiple institutions in Himachal Pradesh, India. The study included undergraduate and postgraduate medical students, with no specified exclusion criteria beyond participation consent.
Core methods
A structured, self-administered questionnaire was used to collect data on HPV knowledge, attitudes toward HPV vaccination, and vaccination-related practices. The questionnaire covered awareness of HPV, perceived risks, vaccine hesitancy, and prior vaccination status among participants.
Main results
The study reported that 68.4% of participants had adequate knowledge about HPV, while 52.3% demonstrated positive attitudes toward HPV vaccination. Only 34.7% of students had received at least one dose of the HPV vaccine, with significant variability across institutions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a critical need for targeted educational interventions to improve HPV vaccine uptake among future healthcare providers. They highlighted the discrepancy between knowledge, attitudes, and actual vaccination behavior as a novel observation in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of integrating HPV education into medical curricula to address vaccine hesitancy and improve future vaccination practices among healthcare professionals. This could enhance patient counseling and public health advocacy for HPV vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and self-reported data may introduce response bias. The sample was limited to medical students in one Indian state, restricting generalizability to broader populations or other regions.

尼日利亚埃努古市女中学生人乳头瘤病毒感染和 疫苗 意识评估:疫苗接种 推出前调查

Assessment of Human Papilloma Virus Infection and Vaccine Awareness among Female Secondary School Students in Enugu Metropolis, Nigeria: A Pre-Vaccination Rollout Survey
作者： Odimegwu CL, Okoro OS, Nnagbo JP, Chime PU, Mbanefo NR, Adiri CO, Uwaezuoke SN, Nnaji HC, Nkwo PO, Eze MI
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一种可预防的疾病,一般人群中 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染率较高,但之前在尼日利亚和其他非洲地区进行的研究表明,青少年对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的认识较低。本研究旨在在全国 HPV 疫苗接种 推广之前评估埃努古市女中学生对 HPV 感染及其 疫苗 的了解和认识。
设计与对象
2023 年 10 月,对尼日利亚埃努古市 647 名 10 至 19 岁女中学生进行了一项横断面调查。
核心方法
使用结构化、自我管理的问卷收集数据,并使用描述性统计和卡方检验探索知识与人口统计变量之间的关联。
主要结果
参与者的平均年龄为13.45 ± 1.81岁,78%的人从未听说过人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),79.8%的人对HPV 疫苗的认知水平较差。年龄超过13岁的学生及父母受教育程度较高者对HPV的认知显著更高,学校是最常被提及的信息来源(53%)。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,目标人群对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的认识较差,强调需要进行早期学校教育和多部门利益相关者参与,以提高认识和 疫苗接种 的采用率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有必要在学校和社区参与中有针对性的教育干预措施,以消除对 HPV 症状和预防的误解,从而提高 疫苗 的吸收率并减少 HPV 相关疾病负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能引入反应偏差,导致证据强度中等。

Assessment of Human Papilloma Virus Infection and Vaccine Awareness among Female Secondary School Students in Enugu Metropolis, Nigeria: A Pre-Vaccination Rollout Survey

Authors: Odimegwu CL, Okoro OS, Nnagbo JP, Chime PU, Mbanefo NR, Adiri CO, Uwaezuoke SN, Nnaji HC, Nkwo PO, Eze MI
Original abstract (English)
Background: The prevalence of human papilloma virus(HPV) infection is high in the general population and HPV is considered an important cause of cervical cancer which is a preventable disease. However, previous studies done in other settings in Nigeria and other African countries suggested that there was low level of awareness of HPV infection and its vaccine. Objectives: This study assessed the knowledge and awareness of HPV infection and its vaccine among female secondary school students in Enugu metropolis, Nigeria, prior to the National HPV vaccination rollout in October 2023. Methods: A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 647 female students aged 10–19 years in Enugu metropolis in October 2023. Data was collected using structured, self-administered questionnaires. Descriptive statistics and chi-square tests were used to explore associations between knowledge and demographic variables. Results: The mean age of participants was 13.45 ± 1.81 years. Overall, 78% had never heard of HPV, and 79.8% demonstrated poor knowledge of the HPV vaccine. Students older than 13 years and those with more educated parents showed significantly higher awareness. Schools were the most cited source of information (53%). Misconceptions about HPV symptoms and prevention were common. Conclusion: Awareness of HPV and its vaccine was poor among the target demographic, underscoring the need for early school-based education and multi-sect oral stakeholder engagement to improve awareness of HPV infection and its vaccination uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a preventable disease with high prevalence of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in the general population, yet prior studies in Nigeria and other African settings reported low awareness of HPV and its vaccine among adolescents. This study aimed to assess knowledge and awareness of HPV infection and its vaccine among female secondary school students in Enugu metropolis before the national HPV vaccination rollout.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted in October 2023 among 647 female secondary school students aged 10–19 years in Enugu metropolis, Nigeria.
Core methods
Data were collected using structured, self-administered questionnaires, and associations between knowledge and demographic variables were explored using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests.
Main results
The mean age of participants was 13.45 ± 1.81 years, 78% had never heard of HPV, and 79.8% demonstrated poor knowledge of the HPV vaccine. Students older than 13 years and those with more educated parents showed significantly higher awareness, with schools being the most cited source of information (53%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating poor awareness of HPV and its vaccine among the target demographic, highlighting the need for early school-based education and multi-sectoral stakeholder engagement to improve awareness and vaccination uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational interventions in schools and community engagement are necessary to address misconceptions about HPV symptoms and prevention, thereby improving vaccine uptake and reducing HPV-associated disease burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit causal inference and may introduce response bias, resulting in a moderate strength of evidence.

伊拉克埃尔比勒妇女宫颈炎和未确定意义的非典型鳞状细胞病变的 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型:伊拉克埃尔比勒妇女宫颈炎和 ASCUS 病变的 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型

Human papillomavirus genotypes in cervicitis and atypical squamous cell of undetermined significance lesions among women in Erbil, Iraq: Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Cervicitis and ASCUS Lesions Among Women in Erbil, Iraq
作者： Dashty Hamad ameen, Tavga Salim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危(HR)人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)持续感染是宫颈癌的主要原因,但在伊拉克,宫颈病变如宫颈炎和意义不明的非典型鳞状细胞(ASC-US)的基因型分布仍不充分。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究分析了伊拉克埃尔比勒 113 名 20 至 50 岁患有宫颈炎或 ASC-US 病变女性的宫颈涂片,以确定 HPV 患病率 和基因型分布。
核心方法
使用逆转录聚合酶链反应和杂交方法对宫颈样本进行 HPV 检测,然后进行 HPV 基因分型测定以鉴定特定基因型。
主要结果
113例中,HPV阳性率为24.74%,HPV阴性率为75.22%;HR-HPV16 和多种基因型(31、35、39、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68)是最常见的。
解释与创新
该研究报告称,在宫颈炎和 ASC-US 患者中检测到低基因型和 高危型HPV 基因型,强调了它们在宫颈异常中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在伊拉克需要开展 宫颈癌 筛查计划和预防性 HPV 疫苗接种,以减少 HPV 相关的宫颈异常和肿瘤。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于样本量小和横截面设计,限制了因果推断和普遍性;证据强度被评估为中等。

Human papillomavirus genotypes in cervicitis and atypical squamous cell of undetermined significance lesions among women in Erbil, Iraq: Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Cervicitis and ASCUS Lesions Among Women in Erbil, Iraq

Authors: Dashty Hamad ameen, Tavga Salim
Original abstract (English)
Background and objective: Human papillomavirus infection is known to be the primary reason for cervical carcinoma. Steady infection with high risk (HR)-HPV strains is behind most abnormal cervical lesions, this studyaims to investigate the prevalence and genotype distribution of HPV in cervical samples from women between 20 to 50 years old with different cervical lesions, including cervicitis and atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance (ASC-US) cases. Methods: Cervical smears of 113 patients diagnosed with atypical squamous cell of undetermined significance and cervicitis were included in the study and examined for HPV detection used reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction and hybridization method, followed by HPV genotyping assay specimens Results: Out of the total 113 cases, 24.74% were positive for HPV infection, and 75.22% were negative for HPV infection. Most of the patients diagnosed with cervicitis were within 35-39 age group, accounting for 26.3% of the total cases. In contrast, most patients with ASCUS were in the 30-34 age group, representing 29.7%. The majority of patients exhibit a single serotype (60.7%), while multiple serotypes were found in 39.3%. HR-HPV16 and multiple genotype HPV (31,35,39,51,52,56,58,59,66, and 68) were the most frequently identified genotypes. Conclusion: The detection of both low- and HR- HPV genotypes were reported and not uncommon among patients with cervicitis and a typical squamous cell of undetermined significance. High risk (HR)-HPV16 and multiple genotypes were the most frequently diagnosed by PCR test. Consequently, it is highly advisable to create a program for cervical cancer screening and prophylactic HPV vaccination for early diagnosis and prevention of cervical abnormalities and neoplasia in Iraq.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk (HR) human papillomavirus (HPV) is a primary cause of cervical carcinoma, yet the genotype distribution in cervical lesions such as cervicitis and atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance (ASC-US) remains insufficiently characterized in Iraq.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study analyzed cervical smears from 113 women aged 20 to 50 years with cervicitis or ASC-US lesions in Erbil, Iraq, to determine HPV prevalence and genotype distribution.
Core methods
Cervical samples were tested for HPV using reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction and a hybridization method, followed by HPV genotyping assays to identify specific genotypes.
Main results
Of 113 cases, 24.74% were HPV-positive and 75.22% were HPV-negative; HR-HPV16 and multiple genotypes (31, 35, 39, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68) were the most frequently identified.
Interpretation and novelty
The study reports that both low- and high-risk HPV genotypes were detected in patients with cervicitis and ASC-US, highlighting their potential role in cervical abnormalities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the need for cervical cancer screening programs and prophylactic HPV vaccination in Iraq to reduce HPV-associated cervical abnormalities and neoplasia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and cross-sectional design, which restricts causal inference and generalizability; evidence strength is assessed as moderate.

人乳头瘤病毒对宫颈上皮内瘤变血清和宫颈粘液细胞因子谱的影响

The influence of human papillomaviruses on the cytokine profile of blood serum and cervical mucus in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia
作者： A. N. Riger, A.I. Zubkova, И. Б. Антонова, Н. В. Мельникова, A. L. Major, G. G. Khakimova, Н. В. Харченко, А. Д. Каприн, A. Zubková, I. Antonova
摘要中文翻译探讨不同型别人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)及病毒载量水平对宫颈上皮内瘤变宫颈粘液及血清细胞因子谱的影响。材料和方法。该研究包括 30 名健康女性(对照组)和 60 名感染 HPV 并诊断为轻度(LSIL,n = 30)和重度(HSIL,n = 30)宫颈上皮内瘤变的患者。使用 Pro Human Cytokine 27-plex Assay(Bio-Rad Laboratories,Inc.,USA)在 Luminex 200 分析仪(美国)上测定宫颈粘液和血清中的免疫调节因子水平。为了评估按病毒存在、HPV 类型和病毒载量水平分类的组之间的差异,使用了单向方差分析 (ANOVA) 和方差不等的双样本 t 检验。在LSIL中,与对照组相比,血液和宫颈粘液中IL-4、IL-8、FGF、IL-1b、IL1-ra、IL-2、IL-5、IP-10、MCP-1和PDGF-BB的水平存在相反且显着的差异。在 HSIL 中,观察到 FGF、IL-10、IL-8、IL-17A、TNF-a 和 VEGF 水平的变化。在所有HPV阳性患者的血清中,无论何种病毒株,与健康女性相比,IL-17A、IL-2、IL-5、IL-9和PDGF-BB的水平均降低,而IL-4、IL-7、IL-10的水平和IL-10/IL-17A比值升高。在所有病毒阳性患者的宫颈粘液中,与HPV阴性组相比,观察到IL-1b、VEGF、FGF水平升高,而IL-8和IP-10水平显着降低。无论病毒类型和病毒载量水平如何,体内 HPV 感染的存在对宫颈上皮内瘤样病变的局部和全身细胞因子谱产生主要影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
致癌性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的持续感染是宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 的必要原因,但 HPV 类型和病毒载量如何影响 CIN 中的局部和全身细胞因子谱仍不完全清楚。该研究旨在确定 HPV 的存在、类型或病毒载量是否会差异性地改变患有 CIN 的女性宫颈粘液和血清中的细胞因子因子谱。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究招募了 30 名健康女性作为对照,以及 60 名 HPV 感染的 CIN 患者,分为 30 名低度鳞状上皮内病变 (LSIL) 和 30 名高度鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL)。
核心方法
使用 Luminex 200 分析仪上的 Pro Human Cytokine 27-plex Assay 对宫颈粘液和血清中的细胞因子水平进行定量。通过单向方差分析和方差不等的双样本 t 检验评估组间差异,以根据 HPV 存在、类型和病毒载量比较细胞因子谱。
主要结果
在 LSIL 中,血液和宫颈粘液中的 IL-4、IL-8、FGF、IL-1β、IL-1ra、IL-2、IL-5、IP-10、MCP-1 和 PDGF-BB 与对照组相比存在显着差异。在 HSIL 中,FGF、IL-10、IL-8、IL-17A、TNF-α 和 VEGF 发生变化。在所有 HPV 阳性患者中,血液显示 IL-17A、IL-2、IL-5、IL-9 和 PDGF-BB 降低,IL-4、IL-7、IL-10 和 IL-10/IL-17A 比率升高;宫颈粘液显示 IL-1β、VEGF、FGF 升高,IL-8 和 IP-10 降低。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,HPV 感染本身,而不是病毒类​​型或载量,是 CIN 局部和全身细胞因子谱改变的主要驱动因素。这是第一项直接比较不同 CIN 级别的配对宫颈粘液和血清细胞因子特征的研究,同时明确测试 HPV 存在的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,血液和宫颈分泌物的细胞因子分析可以作为 CIN 严重程度和 HPV 相关免疫调节的辅助生物标志物,有可能为风险分层和监测策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计排除了因果推断,样本量限制了 HPV 类型或病毒载量的亚组分析。由于依赖单一检测平台且缺乏纵向随访,证据不充分。

The influence of human papillomaviruses on the cytokine profile of blood serum and cervical mucus in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia

Authors: A. N. Riger, A.I. Zubkova, И. Б. Антонова, Н. В. Мельникова, A. L. Major, G. G. Khakimova, Н. В. Харченко, А. Д. Каприн, A. Zubková, I. Antonova
Original abstract (English)
To investigate the influence of different types of human papillomavirus (HPV) and the level of viral load on the profiles of cytokine factors in cervical mucus and blood serum in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia. Material and methods. The study included 30 healthy women (control group) and 60 patients infected with HPV and diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia of mild (LSIL, n=30) and severe (HSIL, n=30) grade. The levels of immunoregulatory factors were determined in cervical mucus and blood serum using the Pro Human Cytokine 27-plex Assay (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Inc., USA) on a Luminex 200 analyzer (USA). To assess differences between groups categorized by the presence of the virus, HPV type, and viral load level, one-way analysis of variance (ANOVA) and a two-sample t-test with unequal variances were used. In LSIL, compared to the control group, opposite and significant differences were found in the levels of IL-4, IL-8, FGF, IL-1b, IL1-ra, IL-2, IL-5, IP-10, MCP-1, and PDGF-BB in both blood and cervical mucus. In HSIL, changes were observed in the levels of FGF, IL-10, IL-8, IL-17A, TNF-a, and VEGF. In the blood serum of all HPV-positive patients, regardless of the virus strain, a decrease in the levels of IL-17A, IL-2, IL-5, IL-9, and PDGF-BB, and an increase in the levels of IL-4, IL-7, IL-10, and the IL-10/IL-17A ratio were found compared to healthy women. In the cervical mucus of all virus-positive patients, compared to the HPV-negative group, elevated levels of IL-1b, VEGF, FGF, and a significant decrease in the levels of IL-8 and IP-10 were observed. The main influence on the local and systemic cytokine profile in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia is exerted by the presence of HPV infection in the body, regardless of the virus type and the level of viral load.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with oncogenic human papillomaviruses (HPVs) is a necessary cause of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN), yet how HPV type and viral load shape local and systemic cytokine profiles in CIN remains incompletely understood. The study aimed to determine whether HPV presence, type, or viral load differentially alters cytokine factor profiles in cervical mucus and blood serum among women with CIN.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 30 healthy women as controls and 60 HPV-infected patients with CIN, stratified into 30 with low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) and 30 with high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL).
Core methods
Cytokine levels in cervical mucus and blood serum were quantified using the Pro Human Cytokine 27-plex Assay on a Luminex 200 analyzer. Differences between groups were assessed with one-way ANOVA and a two-sample t-test with unequal variances to compare cytokine profiles by HPV presence, type, and viral load.
Main results
In LSIL, significant differences versus controls were observed for IL-4, IL-8, FGF, IL-1β, IL-1ra, IL-2, IL-5, IP-10, MCP-1, and PDGF-BB in both blood and cervical mucus. In HSIL, changes were noted for FGF, IL-10, IL-8, IL-17A, TNF-α, and VEGF. Across all HPV-positive patients, blood showed decreased IL-17A, IL-2, IL-5, IL-9, and PDGF-BB and increased IL-4, IL-7, IL-10, and the IL-10/IL-17A ratio; cervical mucus showed elevated IL-1β, VEGF, FGF and reduced IL-8 and IP-10.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that HPV infection itself, rather than viral type or load, is the principal driver of altered local and systemic cytokine profiles in CIN. This is the first study to directly compare paired cervical mucus and blood serum cytokine signatures across CIN grades while explicitly testing the influence of HPV presence.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that cytokine profiling of blood and cervical secretions could serve as adjunct biomarkers for CIN severity and HPV-associated immune modulation, potentially informing risk stratification and monitoring strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design precludes causal inference, and the sample size limits subgroup analyses by HPV type or viral load. The evidence is moderate due to reliance on a single assay platform and lack of longitudinal follow-up.

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-12  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.08.11.26360159  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

模拟吸烟行为对男性到男性 HPV 传播 和肛门癌进展的影响

Modelling the Effects of Smoking Behavior on Male-to-Male HPV Transmission and Anal Cancer Progression
作者： Rashidat Oluwabukola Owolabi, Maia Martcheva, Indrajit Ghosh
摘要中文翻译男男性行为者 (MSM) 中的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染已成为一个重大的公共卫生问题,特别是在无法获得男性 疫苗接种 的国家。鉴于 MSM 对 HPV 和肛门癌的高度易感性,以及低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 男性无法获得 HPV 传播,因此需要确定替代干预措施来减少该人群的疾病 传播 和负担。本文提出的新颖数学模型将吸烟行为动态与 HPV 传播 和 MSM 中肛门癌的进展联系起来。减少吸烟作为一项干预措施被引入,以评估其对疾病 传播 和负担的影响。采用下一代矩阵法推导基本再生数(R 0 ),并利用偏秩相关系数(PRCC)进行全局敏感性分析,以确定模型参数对RR 0 的影响。此外,模型的理论分析揭示了后向分歧,这意味着 RR 0 < 1 对于根除疾病是必要的,但不足以消除。研究发现,相对于没有干预的基线预测,MSM 中吸烟的减少可以减少 HPV 感染和肛门癌负担。减少吸烟和疫苗接种的联合效应表明,随着吸烟减少水平的增加,实现RR 0 <1所需的临界疫苗接种覆盖率降低。减少接触也观察到类似的结果。这些发现强调了同时干预措施的重要性,这可以显着减少 HPV 的传播并减轻高危人群和普通人群的疾病负担。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
男男性行为者 (MSM) 中的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染已成为一个重大的公共卫生问题,特别是在无法获得男性 疫苗接种 的国家。
设计与对象
鉴于 MSM 对 HPV 和肛门癌的高度易感性,以及低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC) 男性无法获得 HPV 传播,因此需要确定替代干预措施来减少该人群的疾病 传播 和负担。
核心方法
本文提出的新颖数学模型将吸烟行为动态与 HPV 传播 和 MSM 中肛门癌的进展联系起来。
主要结果
研究发现,相对于没有干预的基线预测,MSM 中吸烟的减少可以减少 HPV 感染和肛门癌负担。
解释与创新
采用下一代矩阵法推导基本再生数(R 0 ),并利用偏秩相关系数(PRCC)进行全局敏感性分析,以确定模型参数对RR 0 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
此外,模型的理论分析揭示了后向分歧,这意味着 RR 0 < 1 对于根除疾病是必要的,但不足以消除。
局限与证据强度
减少吸烟和疫苗接种的联合效应表明,随着吸烟减少水平的增加,实现RR 0 <1所需的临界疫苗接种覆盖率降低。

Modelling the Effects of Smoking Behavior on Male-to-Male HPV Transmission and Anal Cancer Progression

Authors: Rashidat Oluwabukola Owolabi, Maia Martcheva, Indrajit Ghosh
Original abstract (English)
Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection among men who have sex with men (MSM) has become a significant public health concern, particularly in countries where male vaccination is unavailable. Given the high susceptibility of MSM to HPV and anal cancer, and the unavailability of HPV vaccination for males in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), there is a need to identify alternative interventions for reducing disease transmission and burden in this population. The novel mathematical model presented in this article couples smoking behavior dynamics with HPV transmission and anal cancer progression among MSM. Smoking reduction is introduced as an intervention to assess its effects on disease transmission and burden. The basic reproduction number (R 0 ) is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and a global sensitivity analysis is performed using partial rank correlation coefficients (PRCC) to identify the influence of model parameters on RR 0 . Further, the theoretical analysis of the model reveals a backward bifurcation, implying that RR 0 < 1 is necessary but not sufficient to eradicate the disease. The study finds that smoking reduction among MSM reduces HPV infection and anal cancer burden relative to baseline projections without intervention. The joint effect of smoking reduction and vaccination shows that the critical vaccination coverage needed to achieve RR 0 <1 decreases as the level of smoking reduction increases. A similar outcome is observed for contact reduction. These findings highlight the importance of concurrent interventions, which can significantly curtail the spread of HPV and reduce disease burden in both the high-risk group and the general population.

Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection among men who have sex with men (MSM) has become a significant public health concern, particularly in countries where male vaccination is unavailable.

Given the high susceptibility of MSM to HPV and anal cancer, and the unavailability of HPV vaccination for males in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), there is a need to identify alternative interventions for reducing disease transmission and burden in this population.
Core methods

Main results
The study finds that smoking reduction among MSM reduces HPV infection and anal cancer burden relative to baseline projections without intervention.
Interpretation and novelty
The basic reproduction number (R 0 ) is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and a global sensitivity analysis is performed using partial rank correlation coefficients (PRCC) to identify the influence of model parameters on RR 0 .
Scientific and public-health significance
Further, the theoretical analysis of the model reveals a backward bifurcation, implying that RR 0 < 1 is necessary but not sufficient to eradicate the disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The joint effect of smoking reduction and vaccination shows that the critical vaccination coverage needed to achieve RR 0 <1 decreases as the level of smoking reduction increases.

首次人体、随机、安慰剂对照、双盲 1 期研究,旨在评估健康成人肌肉注射 AAVLP-HPV 疫苗 的安全性和免疫原性

First-in-Human, Randomized, Placebo-Controlled, Double-Blind Phase 1 Study to Assess in Healthy Adults the Safety and Immunogenicity of Intramuscularly Administered AAVLP-HPV Vaccine
作者： Jeanette Prangsgaard, Emma Huus, Jade Alvarez, Richard B.S. Roden, Martin Müller, Qingxin Chen, Preben Bruun Nyzell, John Dirk Vestergaard Nieland, M. Mueller
摘要中文翻译摘要 为了寻找一种简单的 疫苗 来预防所有癌症相关的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),开发了在腺相关病毒样颗粒 (AAVLP-HPV) 表面展示的 HPV16 和 HPV31 的 L2 残基 17-36。在此,一项 1 期随机、安慰剂对照、双盲临床研究已在 20 名男性和女性受试者中进行,采用单剂量水平 (20μg),不含佐剂。AAVLP-HPV 疫苗 给药安全且耐受性良好。使用 AAVLP-HPV 重复 疫苗接种 会在血清中引发中等滴度的 L2 特异性中和抗体。检测到与不同 HPV 类型的 L2 发生交叉反应的抗体,但大多数 疫苗 的反应较弱。我们的结论是,虽然 AAVLP-HPV 疫苗接种 具有良好的耐受性,但可能需要佐剂来持续引发持久且广泛的中和反应。一句话总结 疫苗接种 与 AAVLP-HPV 对健康成人是安全的且具有免疫原性。资助 该研究由 2A Pharma AB 资助。MM 和 RBSR 由 2A Pharma AB 的服务合同提供资金。RBSR 和 JA 由公共卫生服务 (grants.nih.gov) 拨款 P50 CA098252 资助。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种 疫苗,它可以预防所有癌症相关的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),因为 HPV16 和 HPV31 的 L2 残基都显示在 AAVLP-HPV 疫苗 的表面上。
设计与对象
一项 1 期随机、安慰剂对照、双盲临床研究在 20 名男性和女性健康成年人中进行,采用单剂量水平 (20μg),不含佐剂。
核心方法
AAVLP-HPV 疫苗 通过肌肉注射给药,通过监测不良事件和测量血清中的抗体反应来评估其安全性和免疫原性。
主要结果
使用 AAVLP-HPV 重复 疫苗接种 会在血清中引发中等滴度的 L2 特异性中和抗体,但检测到与不同 HPV 类型的 L2 的交叉反应抗体,大多数 疫苗 反应较弱。
解释与创新
作者解释说,虽然 疫苗 安全且耐受性良好,但可能需要佐剂来持续引发持久且广泛的中和反应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,AAVLP-HPV 可能是 HPV 的安全 疫苗 平台,但需要进一步研究来增强其免疫原性以实现更广泛的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单剂量水平和小样本量,由于缺乏长期随访和更广泛的HPV类型评估,证据不充分。

First-in-Human, Randomized, Placebo-Controlled, Double-Blind Phase 1 Study to Assess in Healthy Adults the Safety and Immunogenicity of Intramuscularly Administered AAVLP-HPV Vaccine

Authors: Jeanette Prangsgaard, Emma Huus, Jade Alvarez, Richard B.S. Roden, Martin Müller, Qingxin Chen, Preben Bruun Nyzell, John Dirk Vestergaard Nieland, M. Mueller
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Seeking a simple vaccine to protect against all cancer-associated human papillomaviruses (HPV), L2 residues 17-36 of both HPV16 and HPV31 displayed on the surface of an Adeno-Associated Virus-Like Particle (AAVLP-HPV) was developed. Here, a phase 1 randomized, placebo-controlled, double-blind clinical study has been conducted in 20 male and female subjects at a single dose level (20μg) without an adjuvant. AAVLP-HPV vaccine administration was safe and well tolerated. Repeat vaccination with AAVLP-HPV elicited L2-specific neutralizing antibodies of modest titer in serum. Antibodies cross-reactive with L2 of diverse HPV types were detected, but responses were weak in most vaccinees. We conclude that while AAVLP-HPV vaccination is well tolerated, an adjuvant is likely needed to consistently elicit durable and broadly neutralizing responses. One sentence summary Vaccination with AAVLP-HPV is safe and immunogenic in healthy adults. Funding The study was funded by 2A Pharma AB. MM and RBSR were funded by service contracts from 2A Pharma AB. RBSR and JA were funded by Public Health Service (grants.nih.gov) grant P50 CA098252.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a vaccine that could protect against all cancer-associated human papillomaviruses (HPV), as L2 residues from both HPV16 and HPV31 were displayed on the surface of an AAVLP-HPV vaccine.
Design and population
A phase 1 randomized, placebo-controlled, double-blind clinical study was conducted in 20 male and female healthy adults at a single dose level (20μg) without an adjuvant.
Core methods
The AAVLP-HPV vaccine was administered intramuscularly, and its safety and immunogenicity were assessed through monitoring adverse events and measuring antibody responses in serum.
Main results
Repeat vaccination with AAVLP-HPV elicited L2-specific neutralizing antibodies of modest titer in serum, but cross-reactive antibodies with L2 of diverse HPV types were detected, with weak responses in most vaccinees.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that while the vaccine was safe and well tolerated, an adjuvant is likely needed to consistently elicit durable and broadly neutralizing responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that AAVLP-HPV could be a safe vaccine platform for HPV, but further research is needed to enhance its immunogenicity for broader protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single dose level and a small sample size, and the evidence is moderate due to the lack of long-term follow-up and broader HPV type evaluation.

将 HPV 自我采样纳入产前护理:泰国孕妇的 患病率、基因型分布、临床结果和预防意义

Integrating HPV self-sampling into antenatal care: prevalence, genotype distribution, clinical outcomes, and preventive implications among pregnant women in Thailand
作者： Saifon Chawanpaiboon, Vitaya Titapant, Irene Ruengkhachorn, Chenchit Pichailuck, Chanon Neungton, Nattanan Sarujikamjornwattana, Papasorn Wattanakul, Patthiya Posew, Tham Santilinon, Patcharaporn Thaisuriyo
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 宫颈癌 仍然是全球主要的健康负担,特别是在筛查覆盖率有限的低收入和中等收入国家。怀孕提供了将预防性干预措施纳入产前护理 (ANC) 的机会。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样可以通过减少传统筛查的障碍来提高参与度。目的 本研究评估了泰国孕妇中 HPV 患病率、基因型分布、临床结果以及 ANC 中 HPV 自我采样的可行性。研究设计:这项前瞻性观察性研究纳入了在泰国曼谷 Siriraj 医院 ANC 就诊的 18 岁以上、孕龄 12 周以上的孕妇。数据收集时间为2025年7月21日至2026年1月10日。在标准化指导后,参与者进行了阴道HPV自我采样。使用多重实时聚合酶链反应检测 HPV 并进行基因分型。结果包括 HPV 患病率、HPV 阳性参与者的基因型分布、接受度和随访。结果 644 名孕妇中,147 名检测出 HPV 感染(总体 患病率,22.8%)。在 HPV 阳性参与者中,37 例(25.2%)检测出 HPV16/18 感染,110 例(74.8%)检测出非 16/18 感染。HPV52 是最常检测到的基因型,其次是 HPV16、HPV66、HPV51 和 HPV68。母亲年龄较小、收入较低、离婚/分居/丧偶状态以及吸烟相关暴露与 HPV 感染独立相关。值得注意的是,62.4% 的参与者从未接受过 宫颈癌 筛查。自采样非常容易被接受,主要是因为它的方便性和易用性。结论 将 HPV 自我采样纳入 ANC 中在孕妇中是可行且可接受的。ANC 可能会将 宫颈癌 筛查范围扩大到以前筛查不足的女性,并支持预防策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在筛查覆盖率有限的环境中,宫颈癌 仍然是全球主要的健康负担,而怀孕为将预防性干预措施纳入产前护理 (ANC) 提供了一个战略窗口。该研究旨在评估泰国孕妇的 HPV 患病率、基因型分布、临床结果以及 ANC 内 HPV 自我采样的可行性。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性研究招募了 2025 年 7 月 21 日至 2026 年 1 月 10 日期间在泰国曼谷 Siriraj 医院接受 ANC 治疗的 18 岁或以上且孕龄至少 12 周的孕妇。
核心方法
在标准化指导后,参与者进行阴道 HPV 自我采样,并使用多重实时聚合酶链反应检测 HPV 并进行基因分型。结果包括 HPV 患病率、HPV 阳性参与者的基因型分布、接受度和随访。
主要结果
在 644 名孕妇中,147 人检测到 HPV 感染(总体 患病率,22.8%)。在 HPV 阳性参与者中,37 例(25.2%)检测出 HPV16/18 感染,110 例(74.8%)检测出非 16/18 感染。HPV52 是最常检测到的基因型,其次是 HPV16、HPV66、HPV51 和 HPV68。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明,将 HPV 自我采样纳入 ANC 中对于孕妇来说是可行且可接受的,特别是在先前筛查覆盖率较低的人群中。他们强调了 ANC 将 宫颈癌 筛查范围扩大到以前筛查不足的女性的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 HPV 自我采样纳入 ANC 可以提高筛查参与度,并支持筛查覆盖率有限的低收入和中等收入国家的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在曼谷的一家三级医院进行的,限制了对其他环境的普遍适用性。由于观察性设计和缺乏长期随访数据,证据强度中等。

Integrating HPV self-sampling into antenatal care: prevalence, genotype distribution, clinical outcomes, and preventive implications among pregnant women in Thailand

Authors: Saifon Chawanpaiboon, Vitaya Titapant, Irene Ruengkhachorn, Chenchit Pichailuck, Chanon Neungton, Nattanan Sarujikamjornwattana, Papasorn Wattanakul, Patthiya Posew, Tham Santilinon, Patcharaporn Thaisuriyo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract BACKGROUND Cervical cancer remains a major global health burden, particularly in low- and middle-income countries with limited screening coverage. Pregnancy offers an opportunity to integrate preventive interventions into antenatal care (ANC). Human papillomavirus (HPV) self-sampling may improve participation by reducing barriers to conventional screening. OBJECTIVE This study evaluated HPV prevalence, genotype distribution, clinical outcomes, and the feasibility of HPV self-sampling in ANC among pregnant women in Thailand. STUDY DESIGN: This prospective observational study enrolled pregnant women aged ≥ 18 years with a gestational age ≥ 12 weeks attending ANC at Siriraj Hospital, Bangkok, Thailand. Data were collected from July 21, 2025, to January 10, 2026. After standardized instruction, participants performed vaginal HPV self-sampling. HPV was detected and genotyped using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction. Outcomes included HPV prevalence, genotype distribution, acceptance, and follow-up among HPV-positive participants. RESULTS Of 644 pregnant women, HPV infection was detected in 147 (overall prevalence, 22.8%). Among HPV-positive participants, HPV16/18 infection was identified in 37 cases (25.2%) and non-16/18 infection in 110 cases (74.8%). HPV52 was the most frequently detected genotype, followed by HPV16, HPV66, HPV51, and HPV68. Younger maternal age, lower income, divorced/separated/widowed status, and smoking-related exposure were independently associated with HPV infection. Notably, 62.4% of participants had never undergone cervical cancer screening. Self-sampling was highly acceptable, mainly for its convenience and ease of use. CONCLUSION HPV self-sampling integrated into ANC was feasible and acceptable among pregnant women. ANC may extend cervical cancer screening to previously underscreened women and support prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major global health burden in settings with limited screening coverage, and pregnancy offers a strategic window to integrate preventive interventions into antenatal care (ANC). The study aimed to evaluate HPV prevalence, genotype distribution, clinical outcomes, and the feasibility of HPV self-sampling within ANC among pregnant women in Thailand.
Design and population
This prospective observational study enrolled pregnant women aged 18 years or older with a gestational age of at least 12 weeks attending ANC at Siriraj Hospital, Bangkok, Thailand, between July 21, 2025, and January 10, 2026.
Core methods
After standardized instruction, participants performed vaginal HPV self-sampling, and HPV was detected and genotyped using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction. Outcomes included HPV prevalence, genotype distribution, acceptance, and follow-up among HPV-positive participants.
Main results
Of 644 pregnant women, HPV infection was detected in 147 (overall prevalence, 22.8%). Among HPV-positive participants, HPV16/18 infection was identified in 37 cases (25.2%) and non-16/18 infection in 110 cases (74.8%). HPV52 was the most frequently detected genotype, followed by HPV16, HPV66, HPV51, and HPV68.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that HPV self-sampling integrated into ANC is feasible and acceptable among pregnant women, particularly in a population with low prior screening coverage. They highlighted the potential of ANC to extend cervical cancer screening to previously underscreened women.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating HPV self-sampling into ANC could enhance screening participation and support prevention strategies in low- and middle-income countries with limited screening coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted at a single tertiary hospital in Bangkok, limiting generalisability to other settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and lack of long-term follow-up data.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

对跨男性个体进行家庭 hrHPV 宫颈癌 筛查的可接受性:定性描述性研究。

Acceptability of At-Home hrHPV Cervical Cancer Screening For Transmasculine Individuals: A Qualitative Descriptive Study.
作者： Mollie E. Aleshire, Rhiannon Huffines, Jennifer S. Tinman, Aubree Croghan, Ratchneewan Ross, Shuying Sha, Jennifer Hatcher
摘要中文翻译背景:与顺性别个体相比,跨男性个体完成 宫颈癌 筛查的可能性较小,因此增加了他们患 宫颈癌 的风险。多维社会生态决定因素可能会导致跨性别者的 宫颈癌 差异。目的:本研究探讨了 宫颈癌 筛查的障碍和促进因素、高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 在家自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查的可接受性,以及跨男性个体中的 宫颈癌 健康促进方法。方法:本定性描述研究了 2022 年至 2023 年影响跨男性个体 宫颈癌 筛查的社会生态因素。通过有目的抽样,使用半结构化访谈对 9 名参与者进行了深入访谈。使用主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:宫颈癌 筛查决策是由社会生态障碍和促进因素决定的,包括烦躁不安、负面医疗体验和排斥性临床空间,以及医疗保健提供者的支持和社交网络鼓励。家庭 hrHPV 自我采样是有条件接受的,并且重视自主性和隐私性。肯定沟通和具有文化能力的医疗保健提供者是有效 宫颈癌 筛查推广的核心。结论:解决这些发现需要一种方法,将医疗保健提供者和员工 LGBTQ+ 特定培训、文化谦逊、以患者为中心的护理、确认医疗保健提供者沟通、有针对性的 LGBTQ+ 特定教育、hrHPV 自我采样选项、包容性临床环境、宫颈癌 筛查成本和覆盖解决方案以及通过解剖学识别 宫颈癌 筛查资格的医疗保健系统相结合。通过解决多层次的社会生态障碍,同时扩大患者识别的促进因素,例如自主性、隐私、值得信赖的提供者建议和社会支持;医疗保健提供者和系统可以改善 宫颈癌 筛查的公平性,并减少跨男性和其他筛查不足人群之间可预防的 宫颈癌 差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
跨男性个体的 宫颈癌 筛查完成率低于顺性别个体,导致 宫颈癌 风险升高。该研究旨在确定筛查的障碍和促进因素,评估家庭高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 自我采样的可接受性,并探索该人群中有效的 宫颈癌 健康促进方法。
设计与对象
这项定性描述性研究采用有目的抽样招募 9 名跨男性参与者,进行 2022 年至 2023 年间进行的深入半结构化访谈。采用主题分析来探索影响 宫颈癌 筛查行为的社会生态因素。
核心方法
通过半结构化访谈收集数据,并使用主题分析进行分析,以确定筛查行为的社会生态决定因素的模式。该研究侧重于定性探索,没有实验干预或定量比较。
主要结果
社会生态障碍包括性别不安、负面的医疗体验和排他性的临床环境,而促进因素包括提供者的支持和社交网络的鼓励。由于自主性和隐私优势,在家进行 hrHPV 自我采样有条件地可以接受。
解释与创新
作者解释说,具有文化能力、肯定的医疗环境和提供者沟通对于提高跨男性个体的筛查率至关重要。新颖之处在于以跨男性观点为中心,以确定 hrHPV 自我采样可接受性特有的多层次促进因素和障碍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,整合针对 LGBTQ+ 的提供者培训、以患者为中心的护理和包容性临床政策可以减少筛查不足的跨男性人群中的 宫颈癌 差异。该研究强调需要进行系统级变革,以改善筛查资格识别和成本覆盖。
局限与证据强度
9 名参与者的小样本限制了普遍性,定性设计排除了因果推断。由于依赖自我报告的经验而没有验证或纵向随访,证据强度中等。

Acceptability of At-Home hrHPV Cervical Cancer Screening For Transmasculine Individuals: A Qualitative Descriptive Study.

Authors: Mollie E. Aleshire, Rhiannon Huffines, Jennifer S. Tinman, Aubree Croghan, Ratchneewan Ross, Shuying Sha, Jennifer Hatcher
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Transmasculine individuals are less likely to complete cervical cancer screening than cisgender individuals, thus increasing their risk for cervical cancer. Multidimensional socioecological determinants may contribute to cervical cancer disparities for transgender people. OBJECTIVE: This study explored cervical cancer screening barriers and facilitators, acceptability of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening, and cervical cancer health promotion methods among transmasculine individuals. METHODS: This qualitative description examined socioecological factors affecting cervical cancer screening in transmasculine individuals in 2022 to 2023. Through purposive sampling, 9 participants were interviewed in-depth using semi-structured interviews. Data were analyzed using thematic analysis. RESULTS: Cervical cancer screening decisions were shaped by socioecological barriers and facilitators, including dysphoria, negative healthcare experiences, and exclusionary clinical spaces, alongside healthcare provider support and social network encouragement. At-home hrHPV self-sampling was conditionally acceptable and valued for autonomy and privacy. Affirming communication and culturally competent healthcare providers were central to effective cervical cancer screening promotion. CONCLUSION: Addressing these findings requires an approach that integrates healthcare provider and staff LGBTQ+-specific training, cultural humility, patient-centered care, affirming healthcare provider communication, targeted LGBTQ+-specific education, hrHPV self-sampling options, inclusive clinical environments, cervical cancer screening cost and coverage solutions, and healthcare systems that identify cervical cancer screening eligibility by anatomy. By addressing multilevel socioecological barriers while amplifying patient-identified facilitators such as autonomy, privacy, trusted provider recommendations, and social support; healthcare providers and systems can improve equitable access to cervical cancer screening and reduce preventable cervical cancer disparities among transmasculine and other underscreened populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Transmasculine individuals face lower cervical cancer screening completion rates than cisgender individuals, contributing to elevated cervical cancer risk. The study aimed to identify barriers and facilitators to screening, assess acceptability of at-home high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) self-sampling, and explore effective cervical cancer health promotion methods within this population.
Design and population
This qualitative descriptive study used purposive sampling to recruit 9 transmasculine participants for in-depth semi-structured interviews conducted between 2022 and 2023. Thematic analysis was applied to explore socioecological factors influencing cervical cancer screening behaviors.
Core methods
Data were collected through semi-structured interviews and analyzed using thematic analysis to identify patterns in socioecological determinants of screening behavior. The study focused on qualitative exploration without experimental interventions or quantitative comparisons.
Main results
Socioecological barriers included gender dysphoria, negative healthcare experiences, and exclusionary clinical environments, while facilitators comprised provider support and social network encouragement. At-home hrHPV self-sampling was conditionally acceptable due to perceived autonomy and privacy benefits.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that culturally competent, affirming healthcare environments and provider communication are critical to improving screening uptake among transmasculine individuals. The novelty lies in centering transmasculine perspectives to identify multilevel facilitators and barriers specific to hrHPV self-sampling acceptability.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings suggest that integrating LGBTQ+-specific provider training, patient-centered care, and inclusive clinical policies could reduce cervical cancer disparities in underscreened transmasculine populations. The study highlights the need for system-level changes to improve screening eligibility identification and cost coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size of 9 participants limits generalizability, and the qualitative design precludes causal inference. Evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on self-reported experiences without validation or longitudinal follow-up.
