焦点:双错配 crRNA 策略利用 Cas12a 解锁高保真一步 SNV 检测。

FOCUS: A Dual-Mismatch crRNA Strategy Unlocks High-Fidelity One-Step SNV Detection with Cas12a.
作者： Miaojin Zhou, Kun Du, Mingjun Jiang, Xin Xu, Xinran Su, Yixi Xie, Dongli Zhang, Xiaoyun Sun, Guangnan Peng, Kun Xia
摘要中文翻译CRISPR/Cas12a已成为核酸检测的强大工具;然而,其临床实用性受到单核苷酸变异 (SNV) 识别的内在限制、对预处理单链 DNA (ssDNA) 模板的依赖以及繁琐的多步骤工作流程的严重阻碍。在这里,我们报告了一种新颖的分子设计原理,通过对具有位点特异性双错配(相对于 PAM 的位置 12 和 14)的 crRNA 进行工程改造,我们系统地证明了该原理可以显着增强 Cas12a 的 SNV 辨别能力。利用这一突破,我们开发了 FOCUS(基于 CRISPR 的快速通用通用 SNV 检测系统),这是一种一体化 CRISPR 传感平台,通过将扩增和检测集成在单个反应系统中,能够对双链 DNA (dsDNA) 中的 SNV 进行等温检测。FOCUS 实现了阿摩尔级灵敏度 (13.15 aM) 和超快读数 (< 20 分钟),可区分高度同源的运动神经元存活基因 1 (SMN1) 和 SMN2 基因,这是脊髓性肌萎缩症 (SMA) 诊断中 SNV 基因分型的金标准挑战。为了验证其临床可转化性,FOCUS 成功适应了使用侧流条和紫外线可视化的低成本、无设备检测,促进了即时检测 (POCT)。在对 175 个临床样本的全面验证中,FOCUS 对 SMA(21 个样本)、高危型HPV 16/18(27 个样本)、金黄色葡萄球菌(20 个样本)和 SARS-CoV-2(107 个样本)表现出与金标准方法 100% 的诊断一致性。总的来说,我们的研究为 Cas12a crRNA 建立了一个通用的工程策略,并将 FOCUS 作为一种强大的、多功能的、可现场部署的解决方案,用于精确的 SNV 基因分型,强调了 FOCUS 在分子诊断中的转化医学价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 CRISPR/Cas12a 在高保真度检测单核苷酸变异 (SNV) 方面的局限性,特别是在临床环境中,准确的 SNV 区分对于诊断 高危型HPV 感染等疾病至关重要。这一背景凸显了CRISPR/Cas12a的临床应用因其无法有效区分SNV而受到阻碍。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对 175 个临床样本(包括来自 高危型HPV 16/18 的样本)进行全面验证,以评估 FOCUS 平台的性能。这些样本是从不同来源收集的,并测试了它们检测与这些 HPV 类型相关的 SNV 的能力。
核心方法
这些方法包括开发 FOCUS,这是一个基于 CRISPR 的平台,将扩增和检测集成在单个反应系统中。该平台使用低成本、无设备的检测进行了验证,该检测采用侧流试纸条和紫外线可视化,适用于即时检测 (POCT)。
主要结果
FOCUS 与 高危型HPV 16/18 的黄金标准方法具有 100% 的诊断一致性,表明其在这些情况下准确检测 SNV 的有效性。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为一种新颖的分子设计原理的论证,该原理通过 crRNA 中的位点特异性双错配来增强 Cas12a 的 SNV 辨别能力。这种方法被认为是分子诊断领域的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于提高 HPV 相关诊断中 SNV 检测的准确性和效率具有重要意义,有助于早期检测和管理 高危型HPV 感染及相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于对 175 个临床样本的全面验证,这为 FOCUS 用于 高危型HPV 检测的性能提供了一定的信心。然而,该研究没有报告潜在的局限性,例如测试样本之外的偏见或普遍性。

FOCUS: A Dual-Mismatch crRNA Strategy Unlocks High-Fidelity One-Step SNV Detection with Cas12a.

Authors: Miaojin Zhou, Kun Du, Mingjun Jiang, Xin Xu, Xinran Su, Yixi Xie, Dongli Zhang, Xiaoyun Sun, Guangnan Peng, Kun Xia
Original abstract (English)
CRISPR/Cas12a has emerged as a powerful tool for nucleic acid detection; however, its clinical utility is severely hampered by intrinsic limitations in single-nucleotide variant (SNV) discrimination, reliance on pre-processed single-stranded DNA (ssDNA) templates, and cumbersome multi-step workflows. Here, we report a novel molecular design principle by engineering crRNAs with site-specific dual mismatches (positions 12 and 14 relative to the PAM), which we systematically demonstrate to drastically enhance the SNV discrimination capability of Cas12a. Leveraging this breakthrough, we developed FOCUS (Fast One-step CRISPR-based Universalizable SNV detection system), an all-in-one CRISPR sensing platform that enables isothermal detection of SNVs from double-stranded DNA (dsDNA) by integrating amplification and detection in a single reaction system. FOCUS achieved attomolar-level sensitivity (13.15 aM) and ultrafast readout (< 20 min) for distinguishing the highly homologous survival motor neuron 1 (SMN1) and SMN2 genes-the gold standard challenge for SNV genotyping in spinal muscular atrophy (SMA) diagnostics. To validate its clinical translatability, FOCUS was successfully adapted to a low-cost, equipment-free assay using lateral flow strips and UV visualization, facilitating point-of-care testing (POCT). In a comprehensive validation across 175 clinical samples, FOCUS exhibited 100% diagnostic concordance with gold-standard methods for SMA (21 samples), high-risk HPV 16/18 (27 samples), Staphylococcus aureus (20 samples), and SARS-CoV-2 (107 samples). Collectively, our study establishes a generalizable engineering strategy for Cas12a crRNAs and presents FOCUS as a robust, versatile, and field-deployable solution for precision SNV genotyping, underscoring the translational medicine value of FOCUS in molecular diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of CRISPR/Cas12a in detecting single-nucleotide variants (SNVs) with high fidelity, particularly in clinical settings where accurate SNV discrimination is critical for diagnosing diseases like high-risk HPV infections. The background highlights that CRISPR/Cas12a's clinical utility is hampered by its inability to distinguish SNVs effectively.
Design and population
The study involved a comprehensive validation across 175 clinical samples, including those from high-risk HPV 16/18, to assess the performance of the FOCUS platform. The samples were collected from various sources and tested for their ability to detect SNVs associated with these HPV types.
Core methods
The methods included the development of FOCUS, a CRISPR-based platform that integrates amplification and detection in a single reaction system. The platform was validated using a low-cost, equipment-free assay with lateral flow strips and UV visualization, which is suitable for point-of-care testing (POCT).
Main results
FOCUS demonstrated 100% diagnostic concordance with gold-standard methods for high-risk HPV 16/18, indicating its effectiveness in accurately detecting SNVs in these contexts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as a demonstration of a novel molecular design principle that enhances the SNV discrimination capability of Cas12a through site-specific dual mismatches in crRNAs. This approach is presented as a significant advancement in the field of molecular diagnostics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have important implications for improving the accuracy and efficiency of SNV detection in HPV-related diagnostics, which can aid in early detection and management of high-risk HPV infections and associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on a comprehensive validation across 175 clinical samples, which provides moderate confidence in the performance of FOCUS for high-risk HPV detection. However, the study does not report on potential limitations such as bias or generalisability beyond the tested samples.

TP53、PIK3CA 和 CCND1 的体细胞改变揭示了口腔和口咽肿瘤中暴露驱动的模式。

Somatic alterations in TP53, PIK3CA, and CCND1 reveal exposure-driven patterns in oral and oropharyngeal tumors.
作者： Ricardo Cortez Cardoso Penha, Shama Virani, Jeremiah R Holt, Xiaobei Zhao, Paul Little, Myriam Brossard, Valérie Gaborieau, Maja Milojevic, Laura Torrens, Hanla A. Park
摘要中文翻译摘要:烟草、酒精和人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是口腔癌(OC)和口咽癌(OPC)的主要危险因素,但它们对肿瘤分子景观的具体贡献尚未完全明确。我们分析了1,086例肿瘤(606例OC、480例OPC,其中包括287例HPV阳性OPC)的体细胞突变,这些肿瘤采用包含1,109个基因的检测面板进行分析,并评估了这些突变与暴露因素的关联。PIK3CA突变广泛存在,但具有情境特异性:HPV阳性OPC以APOBEC驱动的突变和FGFR3突变的共发为特征,而HPV阴性OPC和OC则以3q26/28(PIK3CA/TP63/SOX2)扩增为标志。TP53是HPV阴性肿瘤中突变频率最高的基因,在HPV阴性OPC中,吸烟者的TP53突变频率显著升高,而在OC中,无论吸烟状态如何,TP53突变频率均保持高位。CCND1扩增主要局限于HPV阴性肿瘤,且在报告同时使用烟草和酒精的个体中最为常见。这种联合效应在TCGA口腔癌-口咽癌数据集中得到复现,并在转录组水平上得到反映,即CCND1 mRNA表达在双重使用者中最高。在OC中,对至今最大的来自从未吸烟-从未饮酒患者的肿瘤队列(N=100)进行特征分析,发现这些患者更可能来自女性,且与既往吸烟-饮酒患者(N=353)相比,FAT1和CASP8突变更为富集。综上所述,这些发现界定了按亚部位和HPV状态划分的暴露驱动型体细胞突变模式,并为基于病因学的头颈部癌症风险分层提供了分子框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
口腔癌和口咽癌与烟草、酒精和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 有关,但这些暴露对肿瘤分子改变的具体贡献仍不清楚,需要分子表征来告知风险分层。
设计与对象
这项研究使用 1,109 个基因组分析了 1,086 个口腔和口咽肿瘤(606 个口腔肿瘤,480 个口咽肿瘤,包括 287 个 HPV 阳性口咽癌)的体细胞改变,以评估与暴露的关联。
核心方法
通过 1,109 个基因组对肿瘤样本进行分析,以识别体细胞改变,并对分子改变与烟草、酒精和 HPV 状态等暴露之间的关联进行统计评估。
主要结果
PIK3CA 改变很常见,但与具体情况有关:HPV 阳性口咽癌显示 APOBEC 驱动的 突变 和 FGFR3 共现,而 HPV 阴性肿瘤和口腔癌则显示 3q26/28 扩增;TP53 在 HPV 阴性肿瘤中最常发生突变,在 HPV 阴性口咽癌吸烟者中最为常见。
解释与创新
该研究通过肿瘤亚位点和 HPV 状态定义了不同的、暴露驱动的体细胞改变模式,提供了一个分子框架,可以指导头颈癌基于病因的风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过将特定的分子改变与口腔癌和口咽癌中的烟草、酒精和 HPV 感染等已知暴露联系起来,为 监测 策略和风险分层提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于使用目标基因组分析的单个 队列研究,并且缺乏功能验证,这限制了因果推断和对更广泛人群的推广。

Somatic alterations in TP53, PIK3CA, and CCND1 reveal exposure-driven patterns in oral and oropharyngeal tumors.

Authors: Ricardo Cortez Cardoso Penha, Shama Virani, Jeremiah R Holt, Xiaobei Zhao, Paul Little, Myriam Brossard, Valérie Gaborieau, Maja Milojevic, Laura Torrens, Hanla A. Park
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Tobacco, alcohol, and human papillomavirus (HPV) are major risk factors for oral cavity (OC) and oropharyngeal (OPC) cancers, yet their specific contributions to the tumor molecular landscape remain incompletely understood. We analyzed somatic alterations in 1,086 tumors (606 OC, 480 OPC, including 287 HPV(+)OPC) profiled with a 1,109-gene panel and evaluated their associations with exposures. PIK3CA alterations were widespread but context-specific: HPV(+)OPC featured APOBEC-driven mutations and co-occurrence of FGFR3 mutations in a subset of cases, while HPV(-)OPC and OC were marked by 3q26/28 (PIK3CA/TP63/SOX2) amplifications. TP53 was the most frequently mutated gene in HPV(-) tumors, with strong enrichment among smokers in HPV(-)OPC, whereas TP53 mutation frequencies in OC were consistently high regardless of smoking status. CCND1 amplifications were largely restricted to HPV(-) tumors and were most frequent in individuals reporting combined tobacco/alcohol use. This combined effect was replicated in the TCGA OC-OPC cohort and mirrored at the transcriptomic level, where CCND1 mRNA expression was highest in dual users. Within OC, characterization of one of the largest cohorts of tumors from never smoker-never drinkers to date (N = 100) showed they were more likely to be from female patients and to exhibit enrichment for FAT1 and CASP8 mutations compared to tumors from ever smoker-ever drinkers (N = 353). Together, these findings define distinct, exposure-driven patterns of somatic alterations by subsite and HPV status, and provide a molecular framework that may inform etiologic-based risk stratification in head and neck cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Oral cavity and oropharyngeal cancers are linked to tobacco, alcohol, and human papillomavirus (HPV), but the specific contributions of these exposures to tumor molecular alterations remain unclear and require molecular characterization to inform risk stratification.
Design and population
This study analyzed somatic alterations in 1,086 oral cavity and oropharyngeal tumors (606 oral cavity, 480 oropharyngeal, including 287 HPV-positive oropharyngeal cancers) using a 1,109-gene panel to evaluate associations with exposures.
Core methods
Tumor samples were profiled with a 1,109-gene panel to identify somatic alterations, and associations between molecular alterations and exposures such as tobacco, alcohol, and HPV status were statistically evaluated.
Main results
PIK3CA alterations were common but context-specific: HPV-positive oropharyngeal cancers showed APOBEC-driven mutations and FGFR3 co-occurrence, while HPV-negative tumors and oral cavity cancers exhibited 3q26/28 amplifications; TP53 was most frequently mutated in HPV-negative tumors with enrichment among smokers in HPV-negative oropharyngeal cancers.
Interpretation and novelty
The study defines distinct, exposure-driven patterns of somatic alterations by tumor subsite and HPV status, providing a molecular framework that may guide etiologic-based risk stratification in head and neck cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform surveillance strategies and risk stratification by linking specific molecular alterations to known exposures such as tobacco, alcohol, and HPV infection in oral and oropharyngeal cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single cohort analyzed with a targeted gene panel and lacks functional validation, which constrains causal inference and generalizability to broader populations.

病例报告:阴茎碰撞瘤,包括 HPV 相关鳞状细胞癌和恶性黑色素瘤

Case report: Penile collision tumor comprising HPV-associated squamous cell carcinoma and malignant melanoma
作者： Luiza Dorofte, Aleksei Zukovets, Sabina Davidsson, Mats G. Karlsson, Gabriella Lillsunde Larsson, Gabriella Lillsunde‐Larsson
摘要中文翻译具有鳞状细胞癌(SCC)和恶性黑色素瘤成分的碰撞肿瘤极为罕见。我们报告了一名 61 岁男性的阴茎碰撞瘤病例,其中包括 HPV 相关的鳞状细胞癌和恶性黑色素瘤。患者接受了部分阴茎切除术和双侧腹股沟前哨淋巴结手术。显微镜检查显示双相肿瘤,其中含有疣状基底细胞样形态的鳞状细胞癌成分,HPV16 和 43 呈阳性,以及与 BRAF 突变 相关的恶性黑色素瘤。由于缺乏针对这种肿瘤类型的精确 TNM 分类和记录的 治疗 指南,该病例给诊断和治疗带来了挑战。

设计与对象
我们报告了一名 61 岁男性的阴茎碰撞瘤病例,其中包括 HPV 相关的鳞状细胞癌和恶性黑色素瘤。
核心方法

科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了诊断和治疗具有 HPV 相关成分的罕见碰撞肿瘤的复杂性,强调需要专门的方法。

Case report: Penile collision tumor comprising HPV-associated squamous cell carcinoma and malignant melanoma

Authors: Luiza Dorofte, Aleksei Zukovets, Sabina Davidsson, Mats G. Karlsson, Gabriella Lillsunde Larsson, Gabriella Lillsunde‐Larsson
Original abstract (English)
Collision tumors with squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) and malignant melanoma components are extremely rare. We report a case of a penile collision tumor with a component of HPV-associated SCC and malignant melanoma in a 61 year-old man. The patient underwent a partial penectomy and bilateral inguinal sentinel lymph node surgery. Microscopy showed a biphasic tumor with a component of SCC with warty-basaloid morphology, positive for HPV16 and 43, and a malignant melanoma associated with a BRAF mutation. This case posed diagnostic and therapeutic challenges due to the absence of a precise TNM classification and documented treatment guidelines for this tumor type.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Collision tumors with squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) and malignant melanoma components are extremely rare.
Design and population
We report a case of a penile collision tumor with a component of HPV-associated SCC and malignant melanoma in a 61 year-old man.
Core methods
The patient underwent a partial penectomy and bilateral inguinal sentinel lymph node surgery.
Main results
Microscopy showed a biphasic tumor with a component of SCC with warty-basaloid morphology, positive for HPV16 and 43, and a malignant melanoma associated with a BRAF mutation.
Interpretation and novelty
This case posed diagnostic and therapeutic challenges due to the absence of a precise TNM classification and documented treatment guidelines for this tumor type.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the complexity of diagnosing and treating rare collision tumors with HPV-associated components, emphasizing the need for specialized approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited to a single case report, which restricts generalizability and the ability to establish definitive treatment protocols.

多发性硬化症女性疾病修饰治疗的妇科并发症。

Gynecologic complications of disease-modifying therapy in women with multiple sclerosis.
作者： Alexandra Balshi, John P Dempsey, Yanli Zhang-James, Riley Bove
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
多发性硬化症 (MS) 疾病修饰疗法 (DMT) 对妇科健康的长期影响仍未得到充分研究,特别是其与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关并发症的关系。
设计与对象
使用多中心数据库评估 DMT 是否与 MS 女性妇科并发症相关,在倾向评分匹配后对 115,522 名女性进行了分析。
核心方法
该研究使用倾向评分 1:1 匹配来比较患有多发性硬化症的女性​​接受 DMT(任何与无)和免疫抑制水平(中度至高与低)的情况,并根据年龄、婚姻状况、种族、民族、烟草使用、体重指数、HPV 疫苗接种 和妇科检查/筛查进行调整。计算风险比 (RR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 进行比较。
主要结果
The DMT (vs no DMT) group had increased risks of HPV positivity (RR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.35-1.72), cervical dysplasia (RR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.31-1.49), cervical cancer (RR: 1.23, 95% CI: 1.03-1.46), cervicitis (RR: 1.25, 95% CI: 1.10-1.41), herpes simplex virus positivity (RR: 1.29, 95% CI: 1.14-1.45), vulvovaginitis (RR: 1.30, 95% CI: 1.23-1.37), and Bartholin cysts/abscesses (RR: 1.37, 95% CI: 1.07-1.76).
解释与创新
MS DMT,尤其是免疫抑制性更强的 DMT,会增加多种妇科并发症的风险,包括与 HPV 相关的并发症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调临床医生需要认识到 DMT 潜在的妇科后遗症,并将妇科咨询和筛查纳入多发性硬化症护理中,特别是针对 HPV 相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对数据库和倾向得分匹配的依赖而受到限制,这可能无法完全解释未测量的混杂因素。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Gynecologic complications of disease-modifying therapy in women with multiple sclerosis.

Authors: Alexandra Balshi, John P Dempsey, Yanli Zhang-James, Riley Bove
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND/OBJECTIVES: The long-term implications of multiple sclerosis (MS) disease-modifying therapies (DMTs) on gynecologic health remain understudied. We leverage a multicenter database to evaluate whether DMTs are associated with such complications. METHODS: We propensity-score 1:1 matched women with MS on (1) any versus no or (2) moderate- to high- (vs low-) immunosuppressive DMT by age, marital status, race, ethnicity, tobacco use, body mass index, human papilloma virus (HPV) vaccination, and gynecologic examinations/screenings. We then compared matched cohorts with risk ratios (RRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs). RESULTS: After matching, we analyzed 115,522 women with MS. The DMT (vs no DMT) group had increased risks of HPV positivity (RR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.35-172), cervical dysplasia (RR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.31-1.49), cervical cancer (RR: 1.23, 95% CI: 1.03-1.46), cervicitis (RR: 1.25, 95% CI: 1.10-1.41), herpes simplex virus positivity (RR: 1.29, 95% CI: 1.14-1.45), vulvovaginitis (RR: 1.30, 95% CI: 1.23-1.37), and Bartholin cysts/abscesses (RR: 1.37, 95% CI: 1.07-1.76). Moderate- to high- (vs low-) immunosuppressive. DISCUSSION: MS DMTs, especially more immunosuppressive ones, are associated with increased risks of several gynecologic complications. Clinicians should recognize these potential sequelae of DMTs and integrate gynecological counseling/screening into MS care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The long-term implications of multiple sclerosis (MS) disease-modifying therapies (DMTs) on gynecologic health remain understudied, particularly regarding their association with human papillomavirus (HPV)-related complications.
Design and population
A multicenter database was used to evaluate whether DMTs are associated with gynecologic complications in women with MS, with 115,522 women analyzed after propensity-score matching.
Core methods
The study used propensity-score 1:1 matching to compare women with MS on DMTs (any versus no) and immunosuppressive levels (moderate- to high- vs low-), adjusting for age, marital status, race, ethnicity, tobacco use, body mass index, HPV vaccination, and gynecologic examinations/screenings. Risk ratios (RRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs) were calculated for comparisons.
Main results
The DMT (vs no DMT) group had increased risks of HPV positivity (RR: 1.52, 95% CI: 1.35-1.72), cervical dysplasia (RR: 1.40, 95% CI: 1.31-1.49), cervical cancer (RR: 1.23, 95% CI: 1.03-1.46), cervicitis (RR: 1.25, 95% CI: 1.10-1.41), herpes simplex virus positivity (RR: 1.29, 95% CI: 1.14-1.45), vulvovaginitis (RR: 1.30, 95% CI: 1.23-1.37), and Bartholin cysts/abscesses (RR: 1.37, 95% CI: 1.07-1.76).
Interpretation and novelty
MS DMTs, especially more immunosuppressive ones, are associated with increased risks of several gynecologic complications, including those linked to HPV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for clinicians to recognize the potential gynecologic sequelae of DMTs and integrate gynecological counseling and screening into MS care, particularly for HPV-related conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a database and propensity-score matching, which may not fully account for unmeasured confounders. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

2018-2023 年美国医疗补助受益人药房和医疗环境中 疫苗接种 的趋势:索赔分析。

Trends in vaccination in pharmacy and medical settings among Medicaid recipients in the United States, 2018-2023: a claims analysis.
作者： Amanda L Eiden, Yi Zheng, Kevin M Bakker, Dong Wang, Marcie Fisher-Borne
摘要中文翻译背景:医疗补助受益人的 疫苗接种 比率通常较低,部分原因是访问权限有限。药房为 疫苗接种 提供易于到达的地点。研究设计和方法:这是对 2018 年 1 月 1 日(COVID-19 大流行之前)至 2023 年 12 月 31 日期间 9 岁以上医疗补助接受者中基于药房的 疫苗接种 趋势的回顾性分析,按年龄和种族或民族划分。确定了在药房施用的所有 疫苗接种 的百分比以及 疫苗接种 比率的总体百分比变化。结果:除一些例外情况外,2023 年总体常规和季节性 疫苗接种 率仍低于 2018-2019 年。在青少年中观察到 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、流感和含破伤风 (Tdap/Td) 疫苗,在不同成人年龄组中观察到甲型肝炎、流感、脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌和 Tdap/Td 疫苗。在研究期间,成人中在药房服用常规和季节性 疫苗接种 的百分比有所增加,其中 19-49 岁人群中含有甲型肝炎、乙型肝炎、HPV、脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌和破伤风的 疫苗 增加了 5.2-17.5 个百分点。在各个年龄组中,基于药房的 疫苗接种 在白人参与者中最常见,其次是黑人,然后是西班牙裔参与者,在 65 岁以上的成年人中差异较小。结论:总体而言,医疗补助受益人的 疫苗 施用率有所下降;然而,在药房服用的比例有所增加。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医疗补助受益人经常面临 疫苗接种 的障碍,包括访问受限,这导致 疫苗接种 比率较低。该研究旨在评估基于药房的 疫苗接种 设置是否可以减轻这些障碍并改善该人群中的 疫苗接种 趋势。
设计与对象
这是对 2018 年 1 月 1 日至 2023 年 12 月 31 日期间 9 岁或以上医疗补助接受者中基于药房的 疫苗接种 趋势的回顾性分析。该研究评估了药房服用 疫苗接种 的百分比以及随时间推移的总体 疫苗接种 比率。
核心方法
该研究确定了在药房服用的所有 疫苗接种 的百分比,并计算了 疫苗接种 比率的总体百分比变化。它按年龄和种族或民族分析了趋势,重点关注常规和季节性 疫苗。
主要结果
大多数 疫苗 的总体常规和季节性 疫苗接种 发病率在 2023 年仍然低于 2018-2019 年,包括青少年的 HPV、流感和 Tdap/Td,以及成人的甲型肝炎、流感、脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌和 Tdap/Td。在药房服用 疫苗接种 的成年人比例有所增加,19-49 岁人群中含有甲型肝炎、乙型肝炎、HPV、脑膜炎球菌、肺炎球菌和破伤风的 疫苗 的比例增加了 5.2-17.5 个百分点。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,虽然医疗补助受益人的 疫苗接种 总体比率下降,但在药房服用 疫苗接种 的比例有所增加,这表明通过药房设置的获取情况有所改善。这一趋势在白人参与者中最为明显,在 65 岁及以上的成年人中差异较小。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,扩大基于药房的 疫苗接种 服务可能有助于解决获取障碍并提高医疗补助接受者的 疫苗接种 覆盖率,特别是针对 HPV 和其他常规 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对索赔数据的依赖而受到限制,这些数据可能无法捕获所有 疫苗接种 或解释潜在的混杂因素。证据是中等的,因为它提供了趋势数据,但没有建立因果关系或解决 疫苗接种 的所有障碍。

Trends in vaccination in pharmacy and medical settings among Medicaid recipients in the United States, 2018-2023: a claims analysis.

Authors: Amanda L Eiden, Yi Zheng, Kevin M Bakker, Dong Wang, Marcie Fisher-Borne
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Medicaid beneficiaries typically have low vaccination rates, in part due to limited access. Pharmacies provide easy-to-access locations for vaccination. RESEARCH DESIGN AND METHODS: This was a retrospective analysis of trends in pharmacy-based vaccination by age and race or ethnicity among Medicaid recipients aged ≥9 from 1 January 2018 (before the COVID-19 pandemic) to 31 December 2023. The percentage of all vaccinations administered at pharmacies and the overall percentage change in vaccination rates were determined. RESULTS: With some exceptions, overall routine and seasonal vaccination rates remained lower in 2023 than in 2018-2019. This was observed for human papillomavirus (HPV), influenza, and tetanus-containing (Tdap/Td) vaccines in adolescents and for hepatitis A, influenza, meningococcal, pneumococcal, and Tdap/Td vaccines in various adult age groups. The percentage of routine and seasonal vaccinations administered at pharmacies increased over the study period among adults, with increases of 5.2-17.5 percentage points for hepatitis A, hepatitis B, HPV, meningococcal, pneumococcal, and tetanus-containing vaccines among those aged 19-49. Across age groups, pharmacy-based vaccination was most frequent in White enrollees, followed by Black and then Hispanic enrollees, with smaller differentials in adults aged ≥65. CONCLUSIONS: Generally overall vaccine administration rates declined in Medicaid beneficiaries; however, the proportion administered at pharmacies increased.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Medicaid beneficiaries often face barriers to vaccination, including limited access, which contributes to low vaccination rates. The study aimed to assess whether pharmacy-based vaccination settings could mitigate these barriers and improve vaccination trends among this population.
Design and population
This was a retrospective analysis of trends in pharmacy-based vaccination among Medicaid recipients aged 9 years or older from 1 January 2018 to 31 December 2023. The study evaluated the percentage of vaccinations administered at pharmacies and overall vaccination rates over time.
Core methods
The study determined the percentage of all vaccinations administered at pharmacies and calculated the overall percentage change in vaccination rates. It analyzed trends by age and race or ethnicity, focusing on routine and seasonal vaccines.
Main results
Overall routine and seasonal vaccination rates remained lower in 2023 than in 2018-2019 for most vaccines, including HPV, influenza, and Tdap/Td in adolescents, and hepatitis A, influenza, meningococcal, pneumococcal, and Tdap/Td in adults. The percentage of vaccinations administered at pharmacies increased among adults, with gains of 5.2-17.5 percentage points for hepatitis A, hepatitis B, HPV, meningococcal, pneumococcal, and tetanus-containing vaccines in those aged 19-49.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that while overall vaccination rates declined among Medicaid beneficiaries, the proportion of vaccinations administered at pharmacies increased, suggesting improved access through pharmacy settings. This trend was most pronounced in White enrollees, with smaller differentials in adults aged 65 and older.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that expanding pharmacy-based vaccination services may help address access barriers and improve vaccination coverage among Medicaid recipients, particularly for HPV and other routine vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on claims data, which may not capture all vaccinations or account for potential confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as it provides trend data but does not establish causality or address all barriers to vaccination.

Journal: Preventive medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-11  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ypmed.2026.108641  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 108641
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

从 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染到系统性红斑狼疮中 HPV 相关癌症:系统评价和荟萃分析。

From human papillomavirus (HPV) infection to HPV-associated cancers in systemic lupus erythematosus: A systematic review and Meta-analysis.
作者： Hongli Wang, Huike Ma, Ruonan Li, Mingzhu Wang, Lin Huang, Jingxia Zhao
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了系统性红斑狼疮 (SLE) 患者的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和 HPV 相关癌症,重点关注 SLE 是否会增加宫颈以外 HPV 感染和 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤的风险这一核心问题。
证据基础与方法
这是一项系统综述和荟萃分析,检索了截至 2025 年 8 月 8 日的 PubMed、Embase 和 Cochrane 图书馆,使用预定义的资格标准并在 PROSPERO 上注册 (CRD420251122192)。计算比值比 (OR) 和具有 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的标准化 发病率 比率 (SIR) 以量化风险。
共识与结论
SLE is associated with significantly increased odds of HPV infection (OR = 4.12, 95% CI 2.03, 8.32) and elevated risks of HPV-associated malignancies, including cervical cancer (SIR = 2.25, 95% CI 1.75, 2.89) and extra-cervical anogenital cancers such as vulvar/vaginal cancers (SIR = 5.60, 95% CI 3.71, 8.43).
争议与缺口
该综述强调,当前以宫颈为重点的 SLE 筛查模式可能低估了宫颈外肛门生殖器癌症的风险,表明临床实践中存在潜在差距。此外,亚组分析表明存在较高风险的亚组(例如,使用环磷酰胺、多个性伴侣、亚洲人群),但这些需要进一步的前瞻性证据进行验证。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调,需要扩大 SLE 患者的筛查策略,将宫颈外 HPV 相关癌症包括在内,特别是外阴/阴道癌,并优先考虑高风险亚组,例如使用环磷酰胺或有多个性伴侣的患者。

From human papillomavirus (HPV) infection to HPV-associated cancers in systemic lupus erythematosus: A systematic review and Meta-analysis.

Authors: Hongli Wang, Huike Ma, Ruonan Li, Mingzhu Wang, Lin Huang, Jingxia Zhao
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The current cervix-focused screening paradigm in systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE) may underestimate extra-cervical anogenital cancer risk. This meta-analysis aimed to quantify the risk of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and HPV-associated cancers in SLE patients. METHODS: A comprehensive search was conducted in PubMed (2010), Embase (2010), and Cochrane Library (2010) to August 8, 2025. Odds Ratios (ORs) and Standardized Incidence Ratios (SIRs) with 95% Confidence Intervals (CIs) were calculated. The study protocol was registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251122192). RESULTS: SLE was associated with significantly increased odds of HPV infection (OR = 4.12, 95% CI 2.03, 8.32). We further assessed the risk of HPV-associated malignancies in SLE patients. SLE patients exhibited substantially elevated risks across multiple HPV-associated cancers (SIR = 1.90, 95% CI 1.51, 2.38). The risk of cervical cancer was more than doubled (SIR = 2.25, 95% CI 1.75, 2.89). Notably, even higher risks were observed for extra-cervical anogenital cancers, namely vulvar/vaginal cancers (SIR = 5.60, 95% CI 3.71, 8.43). CONCLUSIONS: SLE is associated with elevated risks of HPV infection and a broad spectrum of HPV-associated cancers. Subgroup analyses suggested that cyclophosphamide use, multiple sexual partners, and Asian populations may constitute higher-risk subgroups. These findings highlight the need for additional prospective evidence to better characterize HPV-associated cancer risks in SLE patients.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and HPV-associated cancers in patients with systemic lupus erythematosus (SLE), focusing on the central question of whether SLE increases the risk of HPV infection and HPV-related malignancies beyond the cervix.
Evidence base and method
This is a systematic review and meta-analysis that searched PubMed, Embase, and Cochrane Library up to August 8, 2025, using predefined eligibility criteria and registered with PROSPERO (CRD420251122192). Odds Ratios (ORs) and Standardized Incidence Ratios (SIRs) with 95% Confidence Intervals (CIs) were calculated to quantify risks.
Consensus and conclusions
SLE is associated with significantly increased odds of HPV infection (OR = 4.12, 95% CI 2.03, 8.32) and elevated risks of HPV-associated malignancies, including cervical cancer (SIR = 2.25, 95% CI 1.75, 2.89) and extra-cervical anogenital cancers such as vulvar/vaginal cancers (SIR = 5.60, 95% CI 3.71, 8.43).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that the current cervix-focused screening paradigm in SLE may underestimate extra-cervical anogenital cancer risk, indicating a potential gap in clinical practice. Additionally, subgroup analyses suggest higher-risk subgroups (e.g., cyclophosphamide use, multiple sexual partners, Asian populations), but these require further prospective evidence for validation.
Research and practice implications
The findings underscore the need for expanded screening strategies in SLE patients to include extra-cervical HPV-associated cancers, particularly vulvar/vaginal cancers, and to prioritize high-risk subgroups such as those on cyclophosphamide or with multiple sexual partners.

Journal: Healthcare  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-09  |  DOI: 10.3390/healthcare14162457  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 14, 16, 2457
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

家庭医生对HPV感染和HPV的知识水平和认知度疫苗

Family Physicians’ Knowledge Levels and Awareness Regarding HPV Infection and HPV Vaccines
作者： Fatma Ebru Yurdakul, Ömer Acat, Latife Uzun
摘要中文翻译背景:在初级保健机构中,宫颈癌 筛查和预防高度依赖于医疗保健提供者的举措,特别是在土耳其等国家,HPV 疫苗 尚未纳入国家常规免疫计划。由于 HPV 疫苗 对 宫颈癌 提供超过 90% 的保护,因此初级保健提供者的主动指导至关重要。本研究评估了土耳其初级保健机构中家庭医生对 HPV 感染和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的知识水平和认识。方法:共有 238 名在土耳其工作的家庭医生使用便利抽样方法参与了这项描述性横断面研究。数据是通过 Google Forms 管理的在线调查问卷收集的。该调查包括社会人口统计信息表和 33 项 HPV 知识量表。使用描述性统计、正态性评估、卡方检验、Mann-Whitney U 检验、Kruskal-Wallis 检验和层次逻辑回归分析对数据进行分析。结果:总体而言,81.1% 的参与者推荐 HPV 疫苗,83.6% 的参与者认为应将其纳入国家计划。值得注意的是,只有 15.1% 的参与医生自己接种了 HPV 疫苗,而 40.6% 的未接种疫苗亚组 (82/202) 表示他们正在考虑个人 疫苗接种。HPV 知识水平普遍较高,但当前国家计划的知识水平较低。疫苗 推荐者和女性参与者的知识得分显着较高,而自付费用 疫苗 成为临床推荐的主要障碍。在多变量层次逻辑回归中,控制人口统计数据后,单个知识领域并没有作为独立的预测因子出现,只有当前国家计划的知识显示出边缘趋势(p = 0.066),尽管将知识变量作为一个集体块包含在内显着提高了整体模型的解释力(p = 0.003)。结论:土耳其的家庭医生普遍表现出较高的 HPV 相关知识和较高的 HPV 疫苗 推荐率。这项研究阐明,初级保健倡导是由专业知识和压倒性结构因素的综合影响形成的,而不是作为独立预测因素的单个知识领域。虽然未来的战略必须侧重于发展有针对性的继续医学教育,以弥合特定的知识差距并优化机会咨询,但实现最佳免疫覆盖率最终取决于将 疫苗 纳入国家计划,以减轻自付费用障碍。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查调查了土耳其初级保健机构中家庭医生对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和 HPV 疫苗 的知识和认识,重点关注他们在 宫颈癌 预防和 疫苗 推荐实践中的作用。
证据基础与方法
这是一项描述性横断面研究,对土耳其 238 名家庭医生进行方便抽样,通过在线调查问卷收集数据,包括社会人口统计表格和 33 项 HPV 知识量表;分析采用描述性统计、正态性评估、卡方检验、Mann-Whitney U 检验、Kruskal-Wallis 检验和层次逻辑回归。
共识与结论
土耳其的家庭医生表现出普遍较高的 HPV 相关知识和较高的 HPV 疫苗 推荐率 (81.1%),其中女性参与者和推荐 疫苗 的知识得分显着较高。
争议与缺口
在控制人口统计数据后,个别知识领域并未成为 疫苗 推荐的独立预测因素,只有当前国家计划的知识显示出边缘趋势 (p = 0.066),表明特定知识领域对临床行为的直接影响有限。
科研与实践启示
未来的战略应优先考虑有针对性的继续医学教育,以解决特定的知识差距并优化机会咨询,而国家计划整合对于减少自付费用障碍和提高免疫覆盖率至关重要。

Family Physicians’ Knowledge Levels and Awareness Regarding HPV Infection and HPV Vaccines

Authors: Fatma Ebru Yurdakul, Ömer Acat, Latife Uzun
Original abstract (English)
Background: In primary care settings, cervical cancer screening and prevention are highly dependent on healthcare provider initiatives, particularly in countries like Türkiye where the HPV vaccine is not yet integrated into the national routine immunization schedule. Since HPV vaccines provide over 90% protection against cervical cancer, the proactive guidance of primary care providers is critical. This study evaluated family physicians’ knowledge levels and awareness of HPV infection and human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines in primary care settings in Türkiye. Methods: A total of 238 family physicians working in Türkiye participated in this descriptive cross-sectional study using a convenience sampling method. Data were collected through an online questionnaire administered via Google Forms. The survey included a sociodemographic information form and a 33-item HPV knowledge scale. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics, normality assessment, chi-square test, Mann–Whitney U test, Kruskal–Wallis test, and hierarchical logistic regression analysis. Results: Overall, 81.1% of participants recommended the HPV vaccine, and 83.6% believed it should be included in the national schedule. Notably, only 15.1% of the participating physicians had received the HPV vaccine themselves, whereas 40.6% of the unvaccinated subgroup (82/202) stated they were considering personal vaccination. HPV knowledge levels were generally high, but lower when regarding the current national program. Vaccine recommenders and female participants had significantly higher knowledge scores, while out-of-pocket vaccine cost emerged as a major barrier to clinical recommendation. In multivariable hierarchical logistic regression, individual knowledge domains did not emerge as independent standalone predictors after controlling for demographics, with only knowledge of the current national program showing a borderline trend (p = 0.066), although the inclusion of the knowledge variables as a collective block significantly improved the overall model explanatory power (p = 0.003). Conclusions: Family physicians in Türkiye showed generally high HPV-related knowledge and a high rate of HPV vaccine recommendation. This study clarifies that primary care advocacy is shaped by the combined influence of professional knowledge and overriding structural factors, rather than individual knowledge domains acting as independent standalone predictors. While future strategies must focus on developing targeted continuous medical education to bridge specific knowledge gaps and optimize opportunistic counseling, achieving optimal immunization coverage ultimately relies on integrating the vaccine into national programs to mitigate out-of-pocket cost barriers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines family physicians’ knowledge and awareness regarding human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and HPV vaccines in primary care settings in Türkiye, focusing on their role in cervical cancer prevention and vaccine recommendation practices.
Evidence base and method
This is a descriptive cross-sectional study using convenience sampling of 238 family physicians in Türkiye, with data collected via an online questionnaire including a sociodemographic form and a 33-item HPV knowledge scale; analyses employed descriptive statistics, normality assessment, chi-square test, Mann–Whitney U test, Kruskal–Wallis test, and hierarchical logistic regression.
Consensus and conclusions
Family physicians in Türkiye demonstrated generally high HPV-related knowledge and a high rate (81.1%) of HPV vaccine recommendation, with female participants and those recommending vaccines showing significantly higher knowledge scores.
Controversies and gaps
Individual knowledge domains did not emerge as independent predictors of vaccine recommendation after controlling for demographics, and only knowledge of the current national program showed a borderline trend (p = 0.066), indicating limited direct influence of specific knowledge domains on clinical behavior.
Research and practice implications
Future strategies should prioritize targeted continuous medical education to address specific knowledge gaps and optimize opportunistic counseling, while national program integration is essential to mitigate out-of-pocket cost barriers and improve immunization coverage.

早期年轻食管鳞癌中高危型HPV感染与FANCC杂合变异的共存:病例报告和文献综述

Coexistence of high-risk HPV infection and FANCC heterozygous variation in early young esophageal squamous cell carcinoma: a case report and literature review
作者： Lei Wu, Jiyong Jing, Chenjing Zhang, Wensheng Pan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 食管癌在中国构成了重大健康挑战,目前筛查针对的是 40 岁以上的人群。然而,年轻人群中 发病率 的上升(通常症状不典型)导致 发病率 延迟。迫切需要调查该群体的根本原因。病例介绍 我们报告一例28岁男性,患有早期食管鳞癌,表现为非特异性腹胀和腹泻。使用放大内窥镜和窄带成像来识别病变,并通过内镜粘膜下剥离术(ESD)进行治疗。术后病理证实原位癌。分子分析显示 高危型HPV 16/18 感染,全外显子组测序显示 DNA 修复基因改变,包括杂合 FANCC 变异。对 12 个 30 岁以下患者的 PubMed 病例的回顾强调了不同的和非典型的表现,通常在晚期诊断,预后不良。结论 该病例强调了对具有持续症状和危险因素的年轻人保持临床警惕的重要性,可作为当前基于年龄的筛查指南的潜在补充。根据 HPV 相关癌症的既定标准,RNAscope 的发现(评分 3)高度表明 HPV 驱动的肿瘤发生。还鉴定出种系杂合 FANCC VUS,但未经功能验证,无法从这一发现得出机制结论。这两项发现的共存为未来的研究提供了假设生成线索。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述在年轻个体中早期食管鳞状细胞癌 (ESCC) 的背景下检查了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是高危型 HPV16/18,重点关注 HPV 与遗传易感性(例如 FANCC 变体)的共同感染是否会导致这一被研究人群的肿瘤发生。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括单个 病例报告 和通过未指定的搜索方法确定的 12 例 PubMed 30 岁以下 ESCC 病例的文献综述。该综述综合了临床、内窥镜、病理和分子发现(例如,通过 RNAscope 检测 HPV、FANCC 变异的全外显子测序),但没有报告正式的资格标准、数据库或荟萃分析过程。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,高危 HPV16/18 感染和杂合 FANCC 变异可能在年轻患者的早期 ESCC 中共存,RNAscope 证据(评分 3)表明 HPV 驱动的肿瘤发生。然而,由于缺乏功能验证,FANCC 变异的临床意义仍不确定。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了有关 FANCC 变异在 HPV 相关 ESCC 中的机制作用的尚未解决的问题,因为没有进行功能性研究来确认因果关系。此外,对 12 例 30 岁以下病例的文献综述显示出不同的非典型表现,常常导致晚期 诊断 延迟,强调了该人群的诊断挑战。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要提高对具有持续胃肠道症状和危险因素的年轻人的临床警惕性,这表明当前基于年龄的筛查指南可能还不够。它还呼吁对 FANCC 变体进行功能验证,并进一步研究食管组织中 HPV 驱动的肿瘤发生。

Coexistence of high-risk HPV infection and FANCC heterozygous variation in early young esophageal squamous cell carcinoma: a case report and literature review

Authors: Lei Wu, Jiyong Jing, Chenjing Zhang, Wensheng Pan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Esophageal cancer poses a major health challenge in China, with current screening targeting individuals over 40. However, rising incidence in younger populations, often with atypical symptoms, leads to delayed diagnosis. Investigating the underlying causes in this group is urgently needed. Case introduction We report a case of a 28-year-old male with early esophageal squamous cell carcinoma presenting with nonspecific abdominal distension and diarrhea. Lesions were identified using magnifying endoscopy with narrowband imaging and treated via endoscopic submucosal dissection (ESD). Postoperative pathology confirmed carcinoma in situ. Molecular analysis revealed high-risk HPV 16/18 infection and whole exome sequencing showed DNA repair gene alterations, including heterozygous FANCC variants. A review of 12 PubMed cases of patients under 30 highlighted varied and atypical presentations, often diagnosed at advanced stages with poor prognosis. Conclusion This case highlights the importance of clinical vigilance for young individuals with persistent symptoms and risk factors, serving as a potential complement to current age-based screening guidelines. The RNAscope finding (score 3) highly suggests HPV-driven oncogenesis based on established criteria in HPV-associated cancers. A germline heterozygous FANCC VUS was also identified, but no mechanistic conclusion can be drawn from this finding without functional validation. The coexistence of these two findings provides a hypothesis-generating clue for future research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomavirus (HPV), specifically high-risk types HPV16/18, in the context of early esophageal squamous cell carcinoma (ESCC) among young individuals, focusing on the central question of whether HPV co-infection with genetic predispositions (e.g., FANCC variants) contributes to oncogenesis in this understudied population.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base consists of a single case report and a literature review of 12 PubMed cases of ESCC in patients under 30, identified through unspecified search methods. The review synthesizes clinical, endoscopic, pathological, and molecular findings (e.g., HPV detection via RNAscope, whole exome sequencing for FANCC variants) without reporting formal eligibility criteria, databases, or meta-analytic processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that high-risk HPV16/18 infection and heterozygous FANCC variants may coexist in early ESCC among young patients, with RNAscope evidence (score 3) suggesting HPV-driven oncogenesis. However, the clinical significance of the FANCC variant remains uncertain due to lack of functional validation.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights unresolved questions regarding the mechanistic role of FANCC variants in HPV-associated ESCC, as no functional studies were conducted to confirm causality. Additionally, the literature review of 12 cases under 30 showed varied and atypical presentations, often leading to delayed diagnosis at advanced stages, underscoring diagnostic challenges in this population.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for increased clinical vigilance for young individuals with persistent gastrointestinal symptoms and risk factors, suggesting that current age-based screening guidelines may be insufficient. It also calls for functional validation of FANCC variants and further investigation into HPV-driven oncogenesis in esophageal tissues.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 在喀麦隆的可接受性:系统评价和荟萃分析

Human papillomavirus vaccine acceptability in Cameroon: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Fabrice Zobel Lekeumo Cheuyem, Chabeja Achangwa, Rick Tchamani, Armand Tiotsia Tsapi, Edwige Omona Guissana, Eric Mboke Ekoum, Isabelle Nkwele Mekone
摘要中文翻译本系统综述与荟萃分析旨在估计喀麦隆人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗知识、接种意愿、推荐实践及实际接种率的合并患病率,并识别影响HPV疫苗犹豫的决定因素。方法:自有研究至2025年1月,检索PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science、Embase、Cochrane Library及非洲期刊在线数据库。研究报告遵循PRISMA 2020指南,并已在PROSPERO注册(ID:CRD420261301213)。结果:纳入33项研究。本系统综述与荟萃分析显示喀麦隆HPV疫苗可接受性。在4,993名参与者中,主要为医护人员(n=1,739)及家长(n=2,419),其接种意愿的合并患病率为68.1%(95%置信区间[CI]:57.4–77.2;12项研究;I2=98%)。HPV知识缺乏与近三倍的疫苗犹豫风险相关(比值比[OR]:2.58;95% CI:2.06–3.22)。缩略语 — CI:置信区间 — CS:横断面研究 — EPI:扩大免疫规划 — GLMM:广义线性混合模型 — HPV:人乳头瘤病毒 — JBI:Joanna Briggs研究所 — MeSH:医学主题词 — NR:未报告 — OR:比值比 — PLOGIT:概率-Logit转换 — PRISMA:系统综述与荟萃分析报告优先指南 — PROSPERO:国际系统综述前瞻性注册库 — RCT:随机对照试验 — WHO:世界卫生组织。致谢:无。背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗接种是消除宫颈癌的关键策略。在喀麦隆,HPV疫苗于2020年被纳入扩大免疫规划。然而,有关HPV疫苗可接受性的综合证据仍...
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这项系统评价和荟萃分析旨在估计喀麦隆 HPV 疫苗 知识、疫苗接种意愿、推荐做法和实际接种情况的汇总 患病率,并确定 疫苗 犹豫的决定因素。
证据基础与方法
这项系统综述和荟萃分析检索了 PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science、Embase、Cochrane 图书馆和非洲期刊在线,查找截至 2025 年 1 月的研究。报告了疫苗接种意愿、知识、推荐和 HPV 疫苗 摄取的研究均包括在内。使用随机效应模型计算汇总的 患病率 估计值和优势比。使用 I2 统计量评估异质性。该研究按照 PRISMA 2020 指南进行报告,并在 PROSPERO 中注册 ID:CRD420261301213。
共识与结论
接种疫苗意愿的汇总 患病率 为 68.1%(95% CI:57.4–77.2;12 项研究;I2 = 98%)。HPV 疫苗 知识的汇总 患病率 为 41.3%(95% CI:28.7–55.1;33 项研究;n = 8,175 名受试者;I2 = 98%)。大约三分之二的参与者(67.7%;95% CI:50.7–81.0;8 项研究;n = 1,617)报告推荐 疫苗,但实际采用率为 22.9%(95% CI:6.9–54.5;9 项研究;n = 9,686)。疫苗 覆盖范围为 1.6% 至 90.6%。意愿从2014年之前的74.2%显着下降到2021年后的57.5%。在纳入的参与者群体中,医护人员的知识水平(74.5%)和意愿(77.8%)最高。
争议与缺口
所有汇总估计值均观察到高度异质性,这表明它们应被解释为平均估计值而不是精确值。除了与知识差距的关联之外,该研究没有报告 疫苗 犹豫的具体决定因素。
科研与实践启示
需要解决知识差距并加强针对具体情况的 疫苗接种 战略,以提高覆盖率。该研究强调了有针对性的干预措施对于提高喀麦隆对 疫苗 的接受度和采用率的重要性。

Human papillomavirus vaccine acceptability in Cameroon: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Fabrice Zobel Lekeumo Cheuyem, Chabeja Achangwa, Rick Tchamani, Armand Tiotsia Tsapi, Edwige Omona Guissana, Eric Mboke Ekoum, Isabelle Nkwele Mekone
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to estimate the pooled prevalence of HPV vaccine knowledge, willingness to vaccinate, recommendation practices, and actual uptake in Cameroon, and to identify determinants of vaccine hesitancy. Methods We searched PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, Embase, Cochrane Library, and African Journals Online from studies to January 2025. The study was reported following PRISMA 2020 guidelines and registered in PROSPERO ID: CRD420261301213. Results Thirty-three studies were included. Human papillomavirus vaccine acceptability in Cameroon: a systematic review and meta-analysis. Among 4,993 participants, who were mainly healthcare workers (n = 1,739) and parents (n = 2,419), the pooled prevalence of willingness to vaccinate was 68.1% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 57.4–77.2; 12 studies; I2 = 98%). Lack of HPV knowledge was associated with nearly three-fold higher hesitancy (odds ratio [OR]: 2.58; 95% CI: 2.06–3.22). Abbreviations - CI: - Confidence interval - CS: - Cross-sectional - EPI: - Expanded Program on Immunization - GLMM: - Generalized linear mixed model - HPV: - Human papillomavirus - JBI: - Joanna Briggs Institute - MeSH: - Medical subject headings - NR: - Not reported - OR: - Odds ratio - PLOGIT: - Probit-Logit transformation - PRISMA: - Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-analyses - PROSPERO: - International prospective register of systematic reviews - RCT: - Randomized controlled trial - WHO: - World Health Organization Acknowledgements None. Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a key strategy for cervical cancer elimination. In Cameroon, HPV vaccine was introduced into the expanded program on immunization in 2020. However, synthesized evidence on vaccine acceptability is need...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to estimate the pooled prevalence of HPV vaccine knowledge, willingness to vaccinate, recommendation practices, and actual uptake in Cameroon, and to identify determinants of vaccine hesitancy.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis searched PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, Embase, Cochrane Library, and African Journals Online for studies up to January 2025. Studies reporting willingness to vaccinate, knowledge, recommendation, and uptake of HPV vaccine were included. Pooled prevalence estimates and odds ratios were calculated using random-effects models. Heterogeneity was assessed using the I2 statistic. The study was reported following PRISMA 2020 guidelines and registered in PROSPERO ID: CRD420261301213.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled prevalence of willingness to vaccinate was 68.1% (95% CI: 57.4–77.2; 12 studies; I2 = 98%). The pooled prevalence of HPV vaccine knowledge was 41.3% (95% CI: 28.7–55.1; 33 studies; n = 8,175 participants; I2 = 98%). Approximately two-thirds of participants (67.7%; 95% CI: 50.7–81.0; 8 studies; n = 1,617) reported recommending the vaccine, but actual uptake was 22.9% (95% CI: 6.9–54.5; 9 studies; n = 9,686). The vaccine coverage ranged from 1.6% to 90.6%. Willingness significantly declined from 74.2% before 2014 to 57.5% after 2021. Healthcare workers had the highest knowledge (74.5%) and willingness (77.8%) among included participant groups.
Controversies and gaps
High heterogeneity was observed for all pooled estimates, suggesting that they should be interpreted as average estimates rather than precise values. The study did not report on specific determinants of vaccine hesitancy beyond the association with knowledge gaps.
Research and practice implications
Addressing knowledge gaps and strengthening context-specific vaccination strategies are needed to improve coverage. The study highlights the importance of targeted interventions to enhance vaccine acceptance and uptake in Cameroon.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

HPV 疫苗接种 和 治疗 用于皮肤疾病:无法预防。

HPV Vaccination as Treatment for Cutaneous Diseases: Beyond Prevention.
作者： Junhua Qi, Gang Nie
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 可引起从寻常疣到皮肤鳞状细胞癌 (cSCC) 等各种皮肤疾病。尽管 HPV 疫苗 被许可用于预防肛门生殖器感染,但它们越来越多地用于治疗已确定的 HPV 相关皮肤病变。这篇叙述性综述评估了 HPV 疫苗接种 对顽固性疣、非黑色素瘤皮肤癌 (NMSC) 和免疫功能低下人群的治疗作用。唯一的大型随机对照试验显示对肛门生殖器疣没有任何益处,所有积极证据都来自小样本和异质设计的低质量研究。发表偏见是可能的。主要挑战包括缺乏对致癌 β-HPV 亚型的覆盖以及治疗的标签外性质。未来的方向需要针对皮肤 HPV 类型的下一代 疫苗 和精心设计的试验。在等待可靠的数据之前,治疗性 HPV 疫苗接种 应保持严格的研究状态,并且仅应在临床研究背景下或在明确的共同决策后提供,而不是作为常规临床选择。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 在人类 宿主 中用于治疗已确定的 HPV 相关皮肤病,包括顽固性疣和非黑色素瘤皮肤癌。它解决了 HPV 疫苗接种 是否可以治疗现有皮肤病变的核心问题,超出其许可的肛门生殖器感染预防作用。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,综合了已发表的有关 HPV 疫苗接种 治疗皮肤病的证据。该综述不报告系统检索日期、资格标准或纳入研究计数,也不进行荟萃分析。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,基于唯一的大型随机对照试验,HPV 疫苗接种 对于肛门生殖器疣没有任何益处,并且所有治疗用途的积极证据都来自小样本量和异质设计的低质量研究。发表偏见是可能的。
争议与缺口
主要差距包括目前的 疫苗 缺乏对致癌 β-HPV 亚型的覆盖,以及缺乏支持皮肤 HPV 相关疾病治疗效果的高质量证据。研究设计的异质性和小样本量限制了确定的结论。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议,治疗性 HPV 疫苗接种 应继续处于研究阶段,并且仅在临床研究中或共同决策后提供,而不是作为常规实践,等待精心设计的针对皮肤 HPV 类型的试验的可靠数据。

HPV Vaccination as Treatment for Cutaneous Diseases: Beyond Prevention.

Authors: Junhua Qi, Gang Nie
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes cutaneous diseases ranging from common warts to cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC). Although HPV vaccines were licensed to prevent anogenital infections, they are increasingly used off-label to treat established HPV-associated cutaneous lesions. This narrative review evaluates therapeutic HPV vaccination for recalcitrant warts, non-melanoma skin cancer (NMSC), and immunocompromised populations. The only large randomized controlled trial showed no benefit for anogenital warts, and all positive evidence derives from low-quality studies with small sample sizes and heterogeneous designs. Publication bias is likely. Key challenges include lack of coverage for oncogenic β-HPV subtypes and the off-label nature of therapy. Future directions require next-generation vaccines targeting cutaneous HPV types and well-designed trials. Pending robust data, therapeutic HPV vaccination should remain strictly investigational and should be offered only within the context of clinical research or after explicit shared decision-making, rather than as a routine clinical option.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines therapeutic use of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines for established HPV-associated cutaneous diseases, including recalcitrant warts and non-melanoma skin cancer, in human hosts. It addresses the central question of whether HPV vaccination can treat existing cutaneous lesions beyond its licensed preventive role for anogenital infections.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that synthesizes published evidence on therapeutic HPV vaccination for cutaneous diseases. The review does not report systematic search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts, and it does not perform meta-analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that there is no benefit of HPV vaccination for anogenital warts based on the only large randomized controlled trial, and all positive evidence for therapeutic use derives from low-quality studies with small sample sizes and heterogeneous designs. Publication bias is likely.
Controversies and gaps
Key gaps include the lack of coverage of oncogenic β-HPV subtypes in current vaccines and the absence of high-quality evidence supporting therapeutic efficacy for cutaneous HPV-associated diseases. Heterogeneity in study designs and small sample sizes limit firm conclusions.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends that therapeutic HPV vaccination should remain investigational and offered only within clinical research or after shared decision-making, rather than as routine practice, pending robust data from well-designed trials targeting cutaneous HPV types.

处于衰老和癌症十字路口的肿瘤病毒:分子机制和关键信号通路。

Oncoviruses at the Crossroads of Ageing and Cancer: Molecular Mechanisms and Key Signalling Pathways.
作者： Noor Allehyani, Mohammed Alissa, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A. AlShehri, Ghadah S. Abusalim, Tawfiq N. Juraybi, Meshari A. Alsuwat, Awaji Y. Safhi
摘要中文翻译肿瘤病毒是导致大部分人类癌症的原因,并且已成为 宿主 细胞途径的关键调节剂,该途径也控制着衰老。病毒肿瘤发生、衰老和癌症的融合反映了共同的分子机制,包括基因组不稳定性、表观遗传改变、端粒磨损和慢性炎症。越来越多的证据表明,人乳头瘤病毒、Epstein-Barr病毒、乙型和丙型肝炎病毒以及卡波西肉瘤相关疱疹病毒等肿瘤病毒会主动重编程宿主信号网络,以促进持续感染、免疫逃避和恶性转化。这篇综述综合了目前关于肿瘤病毒如何与衰老和癌症的关键特征相交叉的知识,特别强调了桥接这些过程的信号通路。包括 p53、mTOR、NF-κB、PI3K/AKT 和 Wnt/β-catenin 在内的中央通路经常成为病毒蛋白的目标,导致细胞存活、增殖和衰老失调。这些相互作用不仅促进肿瘤发生,而且加速与衰老相关的细胞功能障碍,例如免疫衰老和炎症。此外,我们讨论了病毒诱导的肿瘤微环境调节和 宿主 免疫应答 如何促进衰老与癌症之间的动态相互作用。了解这些相互关联的途径可以为了解肿瘤病毒作为与年龄相关的衰退和癌症发展的驱动因素的双重作用提供有价值的见解。阐明这些机制可能会揭示预防和治疗老龄化人群中与病毒相关的恶性肿瘤的新治疗靶点和策略。

Oncoviruses at the Crossroads of Ageing and Cancer: Molecular Mechanisms and Key Signalling Pathways.

Authors: Noor Allehyani, Mohammed Alissa, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A. AlShehri, Ghadah S. Abusalim, Tawfiq N. Juraybi, Meshari A. Alsuwat, Awaji Y. Safhi
Original abstract (English)
Oncoviruses are responsible for a substantial fraction of human cancers and have emerged as critical modulators of host cellular pathways that also govern ageing. The convergence of viral oncogenesis, ageing, and cancer reflects shared molecular mechanisms, including genomic instability, epigenetic alterations, telomere attrition, and chronic inflammation. Increasing evidence indicates that oncoviruses such as human papillomavirus, Epstein-Barr virus, hepatitis B and C viruses, and Kaposi's sarcoma-associated herpesvirus actively reprogramme host signalling networks to promote persistent infection, immune evasion, and malignant transformation. This review synthesises current knowledge on how oncoviruses intersect with key hallmarks of ageing and cancer, with particular emphasis on the signalling pathways that bridge these processes. Central pathways, including p53, mTOR, NF-κB, PI3K/AKT, and Wnt/β-catenin, are frequently targeted by viral proteins, leading to dysregulated cell survival, proliferation, and senescence. These interactions not only facilitate tumourigenesis but also accelerate ageing-associated cellular dysfunctions, such as immunosenescence and inflammaging. Furthermore, we discuss how virus-induced modulation of the tumour microenvironment and host immune responses contributes to the dynamic interplay between ageing and cancer. Understanding these interconnected pathways provides valuable insights into the dual role of oncoviruses as drivers of both age-related decline and cancer development. Elucidating these mechanisms may reveal novel therapeutic targets and strategies for preventing and treating virus-associated malignancies in ageing populations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review synthesises current knowledge on how oncoviruses intersect with key hallmarks of ageing and cancer, with particular emphasis on the signalling pathways that bridge these processes.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Increasing evidence indicates that oncoviruses such as human papillomavirus, Epstein-Barr virus, hepatitis B and C viruses, and Kaposi's sarcoma-associated herpesvirus actively reprogramme host signalling networks to promote persistent infection, immune evasion, and malignant transformation.
Controversies and gaps
Elucidating these mechanisms may reveal novel therapeutic targets and strategies for preventing and treating virus-associated malignancies in ageing populations.
Research and practice implications
Understanding these interconnected pathways provides valuable insights into the dual role of oncoviruses as drivers of both age-related decline and cancer development.

美国西班牙裔/拉丁裔女性使用专为家庭收集而设计的设备进行 HPV 自我收集的经验。

HPV Self-Collection Experience in US Hispanic/Latinx Women Using a Device Designed for at-Home Collection.
作者： Lisa Memmel, LaShonda Crane, Youri Hwang, Clair Kaplan, Christine Conageski, Sangini S. Sheth, Kimberly Harshberger, Sigrid Williams, Elizabeth Sutton, Colleen McNicholas
摘要中文翻译背景 宫颈癌 是一种可预防的疾病,覆盖筛查不足和/或风险最高的人群对于消除疾病的目标至关重要。美国的西班牙裔女性的 发病率 率比非西班牙裔女性高得不成比例。客观对 SELF-CERV 研究进行事后分析,了解西班牙裔参与者与非西班牙裔参与者相比的独特经历和偏好,目的是促进和改善对更弱势群体的筛查和随访。方法 入组的 25 至 65 岁女性在模拟家庭环境中使用独特的设备自行采集 (SC) 阴道样本,进行高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 检测。为了进行方法比较,获得了临床医生收集的 (CC) 样本。参与者还完成了可用性和筛选偏好调查。结果 共有 609 名参与者获得同意,其中 603 人使用了 SC 设备。在 603 名 SC 用户中,115 名 (19.1%) 认为自己是西班牙裔/拉丁裔。与非西班牙裔女性相比,西班牙裔女性的年收入显着较低 (p < .001),私人保险比例较低,未投保的人数较多 (p < .001),教育水平较低 (p < .001)。西班牙裔和非西班牙裔女性对 宫颈癌 筛查目的的理解没有显着差异。尽管非主要讲英语的西班牙裔女性更有可能推迟筛查,但在推迟筛查方面没有发现差异(p = 0.22)。据观察,与非西班牙裔女性相比,经济障碍对西班牙裔女性的影响更大(p = 0.016)。诊所内窥镜检查的负面经历是相似的;然而,西班牙裔女性 (78%) 对 SC 的支持率明显高于非西班牙裔女性 (62%) (p = 0.001)。两个人群都报告称,她们更有可能通过家用 SC 设备了解最新的 宫颈癌 筛查,但非西班牙裔女性的程度明显较低 (p = 0.002)。在评估远程医疗在筛查中的效用方面,两个人群都报告了相似的医疗保健活动参与度、尝试新技术的意愿以及当前智能手​​机的使用情况。西班牙裔和非西班牙裔女性对于门诊就诊与远程医疗的偏好几乎持平。结论 促进筛查对于西班牙裔女性尤其重要,她们承担着不成比例的 宫颈癌 诊断和 死亡率 负担,同时比非西班牙裔女性面临更多筛查障碍。经过临床验证且易于使用的家庭 SC 替代方案有可能对该社区产生有意义的影响并改善健康结果。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述在美国西班牙裔/拉丁裔女性 宫颈癌 预防的背景下检查了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是 高危型HPV 类型。核心问题集中于与临床医生采集 (CC) 样本相比,在家自行采集 (SC) 阴道样本进行 hrHPV 检测的可接受性、可用性和障碍,以及这些因素在西班牙裔和非西班牙裔女性之间有何不同。
证据基础与方法
该综述报告了 SELF-CERV 研究的事后分析,该研究招募了 25 至 65 岁的女性,在模拟家庭环境中使用独特的设备自行采集阴道样本进行 hrHPV 检测。获得临床医生收集的样本用于方法比较。参与者完成了可用性和筛选偏好调查。该研究包括 609 名同意的参与者,其中 603 人使用 SC 设备。
共识与结论
与非西班牙裔女性相比,西班牙裔女性的年收入明显较低,私人保险覆盖范围较低,未参保率较高,教育水平较低。西班牙裔女性的经济障碍更为明显。西班牙裔女性对 SC 的认可度 (78%) 显着高于非西班牙裔女性 (62%),两组均表示使用家用 SC 设备及时了解最新情况的可能性更高,但非西班牙裔女性的程度较低。
争议与缺口
该综述并未报告西班牙裔和非西班牙裔女性在理解 宫颈癌 筛查或延迟筛查的目的方面存在显着差异,尽管非主要讲英语的西班牙裔女性更有可能推迟筛查 (p = 0.22)。证据并未报告 SC 设备与 CC 采样相比的长期临床结果或诊断准确性。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要解决财务障碍并改善家庭 SC 设备的使用,以促进面临不成比例的 宫颈癌 负担的西班牙裔女性进行筛查。需要进一步的研究来验证 SC 设备在现实环境中的临床实用性和诊断性能,并评估其对减少 宫颈癌 筛查和结果差异的影响。

HPV Self-Collection Experience in US Hispanic/Latinx Women Using a Device Designed for at-Home Collection.

Authors: Lisa Memmel, LaShonda Crane, Youri Hwang, Clair Kaplan, Christine Conageski, Sangini S. Sheth, Kimberly Harshberger, Sigrid Williams, Elizabeth Sutton, Colleen McNicholas
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical cancer is a preventable disease, and reaching populations who are under-screened and/or at highest risk is essential to the goal of disease elimination. Hispanic women in the US have a disproportionately higher incidence rate than non-Hispanic women. Objective Post hoc analysis of the SELF-CERV study of the unique experience and preferences among Hispanic compared to non-Hispanic participants with the goal of facilitating and improving screening and follow-up in more vulnerable populations. Methods Women 25 to 65 years who were enrolled performed self-collection (SC) of vaginal samples for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) testing using a unique device in a simulated at-home environment. For method comparison, a clinician-collected (CC) sample was obtained. Participants also completed usability and screening preference surveys. Results A total of 609 participants were consented and 603 used the SC device. Of the 603 SC users, 115 (19.1%) identified themselves as Hispanic/Latinx. Compared to non-Hispanic women, Hispanic women, had significantly lower annual income (p < .001), a lower percentage of private insurance with more uninsured (p < .001) and lower levels of education (p < .001). There was no significant difference between Hispanic and non-Hispanic women in understanding the purpose of cervical cancer screening. No difference was noted in delaying screening, although English Not Primary speaking Hispanic women were somewhat more likely to delay (p = 0.22). Financial barriers were observed to be more of an impediment to Hispanic women compared to non-Hispanic women (p = 0.016). Negative experiences with in-clinic speculum exams were similar; however, Hispanic women (78%) expressed a significantly higher endorsement of SC than non-Hispanic women (62%), (p = 0.001). Both populations reported that they would be more likely to stay up to date with cervical cancer screening with the at-home SC device, but non-Hispanic women to a significantly lesser degree (p = 0.002). In terms of assessing utility of telemedicine in screening, both populations reported similar engagement in healthcare activities, willingness to try new technology and current use of smartphones. Hispanic and non-Hispanic women were both nearly equally divided as to their preference for in-clinic visits vs telehealth. Conclusions Facilitating screening uptake is especially critical for Hispanic women, who bear a disproportionate burden of cervical cancer diagnoses and mortality while facing more obstacles to screening than non-Hispanic women. A clinically validated and accessible at-home SC alternative holds potential for meaningful impact and improved health outcomes within this community.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPV), specifically high-risk HPV types, in the context of cervical cancer prevention among US Hispanic/Latinx women. The central question focuses on the acceptability, usability, and barriers to at-home self-collection (SC) of vaginal samples for hrHPV testing compared to clinician-collected (CC) samples, and how these factors differ between Hispanic and non-Hispanic women.
Evidence base and method
The review reports a post hoc analysis of the SELF-CERV study, which enrolled women aged 25 to 65 years to perform self-collection of vaginal samples for hrHPV testing using a unique device in a simulated at-home environment. A clinician-collected sample was obtained for method comparison. Participants completed usability and screening preference surveys. The study included 609 consented participants, with 603 using the SC device.
Consensus and conclusions
Hispanic women reported significantly lower annual income, lower private insurance coverage, higher uninsured rates, and lower education levels compared to non-Hispanic women. Financial barriers were more pronounced for Hispanic women. Hispanic women expressed a significantly higher endorsement of SC (78%) than non-Hispanic women (62%), and both groups reported a higher likelihood of staying up to date with screening using the at-home SC device, though non-Hispanic women to a lesser degree.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report significant differences in understanding the purpose of cervical cancer screening or delaying screening between Hispanic and non-Hispanic women, though English-not-primary-speaking Hispanic women were somewhat more likely to delay screening (p = 0.22). The evidence does not report long-term clinical outcomes or diagnostic accuracy of the SC device compared to CC sampling.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need to address financial barriers and improve access to at-home SC devices to facilitate screening uptake among Hispanic women, who face disproportionate cervical cancer burden. Further research is needed to validate the clinical utility and diagnostic performance of the SC device in real-world settings and to evaluate its impact on reducing disparities in cervical cancer screening and outcomes.

HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌:AJCC 第 9 版 TNM 分期的影像框架

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging
作者： Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC),重点关注影像学在 AJCC 第 9 版系统下的 TNM 分期中的作用,特别讨论分期如何影响 治疗 对 HPV 阳性疾病的决策。
证据基础与方法
该文章是一篇叙述性综述,概述了 AJCC 第 9 版 TNM 分期系统对 HPV 相关 OPSCC 的修改,包括纳入影像检测结外延伸 (iENE) 和晚期局部疾病的更新解剖学定义,同时强调放射科医生在多学科评估中的作用。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,HPV 相关的 OPSCC 在生物学和预后上与 HPV 阴性疾病不同,分期直接影响 治疗 的选择;它还强调了放射科医生在使用 CT、MRI 和 PET 评估肿瘤范围、淋巴结疾病、iENE 和远处转移方面的核心作用。
争议与缺口
该评论指出了解读 iENE 时读者间的变异性和不完善的诊断性能,强调需要高级证据来确定其作为临床 N 类别修饰符的一致可重复的预后价值。
科研与实践启示
该综述优先考虑需要进一步的高级证据来验证 iENE 的预后效用,并呼吁在临床实践中谨慎解释,同时还讨论了指导 治疗 选择和使用基于血液的生物标志物(例如肿瘤组织修饰的病毒 HPV DNA)进行治疗后评估的多学科因素。

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging

Authors: Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is biologically and prognostically distinct from HPV-negative disease, and staging directly impacts treatment. The American Joint Committee on Cancer (AJCC) and Union for International Cancer Control version 9 TNM staging system incorporates unequivocal imaging-detected and/or clinically detected extranodal extension (iENE) into clinical nodal staging and definitive pathologic extranodal extension into pathologic nodal staging. Additionally, it provides more explicit anatomic definitions for advanced local disease (clinical T4) while maintaining T and M category definitions from the eighth edition. Radiologists play a central role in the multidisciplinary team by evaluating endophytic tumor extent, regional nodal disease and iENE, and distant metastasis using CT, MRI, and PET. This article reviews clinically relevant oropharyngeal anatomy and proposes a practical radiologic approach to assigning T, N, and M categories in HPV-associated OPSCC. It emphasizes cautious interpretation of iENE because of interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance, with further high-grade evidence needed to establish its consistently reproducible, incremental prognostic value as a clinical N category modifier. It also highlights multidisciplinary factors that guide selection of surgical resection versus nonsurgical therapy. Posttreatment evaluation is discussed in the context of blood-based biomarkers such as tumor tissue-modified viral HPV DNA. The goal of this article is to provide a practical, staging-focused imaging framework for evaluation of HPV-associated OPSCC. Keywords: CT, MR Imaging, Head/Neck, Neck, Pharynx, Surgery, Human Papillomavirus, Squamous Cell Carcinoma, Oropharynx, Transoral Robotic Surgery, Oropharyngeal Carcinoma, TTMV HPV DNA © RSNA, 2026.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), focusing on the role of imaging in TNM staging under the AJCC version 9 system, specifically addressing how staging impacts treatment decisions for HPV-positive disease.
Evidence base and method
The article is a narrative review that outlines the AJCC version 9 TNM staging system's modifications for HPV-associated OPSCC, including the incorporation of imaging-detected extranodal extension (iENE) and updated anatomic definitions for advanced local disease, while emphasizing the radiologist's role in multidisciplinary evaluation.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HPV-associated OPSCC is biologically and prognostically distinct from HPV-negative disease, with staging directly influencing treatment choices; it also highlights the central role of radiologists in assessing tumor extent, nodal disease, iENE, and distant metastasis using CT, MRI, and PET.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance in interpreting iENE, emphasizing the need for high-grade evidence to establish its consistently reproducible prognostic value as a clinical N category modifier.
Research and practice implications
The review prioritizes the need for further high-grade evidence to validate the prognostic utility of iENE and calls for cautious interpretation in clinical practice, while also discussing multidisciplinary factors guiding treatment selection and posttreatment evaluation using blood-based biomarkers such as tumor tissue-modified viral HPV DNA.

针对非传染性疾病的个性化 疫苗 的最新技术:叙述性回顾

The State-of-the Art of Personalized Vaccines for Non-Communicable Diseases: A Narrative Review
作者： Mario Caldarelli, Pierluigi Rio, Carlotta Renna, Andrea Marrone, Giulia Guazzarotti, Antonio Gasbarrini, Giovanni Gambassi, Rossella Cianci
摘要中文翻译非传染性疾病 (NCD),包括癌症、心血管疾病、神经退行性疾病、自身免疫性疾病和过敏性疾病,在全球发病率和 死亡率 中占很大比例。尽管针对乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 等致癌病毒有几种治疗 疫苗接种 策略,但慢性病毒感染尚未被认为是非传染性疾病。事实上,慢性病毒感染会导致与病毒直接相关的恶性肿瘤的发生和进展。这些考虑因素支持非传染性疾病、传染病和治疗 疫苗接种 之间的联系。最新的技术进步为 疫苗 在预防传染病之外的应用铺平了道路,预示着多种慢性非传染性疾病的创新治疗和预防策略。在这里,我们将回顾针对非传染性疾病的个性化 疫苗 策略的最新技术,重点是用于开发这些策略的各种技术平台,包括 mRNA 和 DNA 疫苗、病毒 媒介、基于树突状细胞的 疫苗、纳米颗粒递送系统和下一代佐剂。该综述旨在论证个性化和抗原特异性 疫苗接种 策略作为主要在肿瘤学领域的精准免疫治疗的一个令人信服的选择,其中基于新抗原的 疫苗 正在开发中。此外,我们还将回顾致耐受性的疫苗接种策略、针对神经退行性疾病和心血管疾病中的病理蛋白和通路的疫苗接种策略,以及基于疫苗的慢性病毒感染的治疗。目前有关 疫苗 的证据表明,有几种方法可以诱导 免疫应答 或产生对疾病的耐受性,并可能改变其病程而不是简单地控制症状。然而,仍有许多挑战有待克服,包括疾病变异性、如何识别合适的靶抗原、制造工艺的复杂性、长期安全性以及与已建立的治疗的整合。凭借免疫学、基因组学、生物信息学和新的传递系统等多个领域的融合,精准疫苗学正在获得动力。因此,预计个性化 疫苗 将成为未来非传染性疾病预防和治疗方法中越来越重要的组成部分。

范围与问题
非传染性疾病 (NCD),包括癌症、心血管疾病、神经退行性疾病、自身免疫性疾病和过敏性疾病,在全球发病率和 死亡率 中占很大比例。
证据基础与方法
尽管针对乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 等致癌病毒有几种治疗 疫苗接种 策略,但慢性病毒感染尚未被认为是非传染性疾病。
共识与结论
事实上,慢性病毒感染会导致与病毒直接相关的恶性肿瘤的发生和进展。
争议与缺口
然而,仍有许多挑战有待克服,包括疾病变异性、如何识别合适的靶抗原、制造工艺的复杂性、长期安全性以及与已建立的治疗的整合。
科研与实践启示
最新的技术进步为 疫苗 在预防传染病之外的应用铺平了道路,预示着多种慢性非传染性疾病的创新治疗和预防策略。

The State-of-the Art of Personalized Vaccines for Non-Communicable Diseases: A Narrative Review

Authors: Mario Caldarelli, Pierluigi Rio, Carlotta Renna, Andrea Marrone, Giulia Guazzarotti, Antonio Gasbarrini, Giovanni Gambassi, Rossella Cianci
Original abstract (English)
Non-communicable diseases (NCDs), including cancer, cardiovascular, neurodegenerative, autoimmune, and allergic diseases, account for a significant amount of global morbidity and mortality. Chronic viral infections have not been recognized as NCDs, despite the availability of several therapeutic vaccination strategies against oncogenic viruses, such as the hepatitis B virus (HBV) and the human papillomavirus (HPV). Indeed, chronic viral infection leads to the development and progression of malignancies directly linked to the viruses. These considerations support a connection between NCDs, infectious diseases, and therapeutic vaccination. Recent advances in technology have paved the way to the use of vaccines beyond the prevention of infectious diseases, heralding innovative therapeutic and preventative strategies for a variety of chronic NCDs. Here we will review the state of the art of personalized vaccine strategies for NCDs, with an emphasis on the diverse technological platforms used to develop them, including mRNA and DNA vaccines, viral vectors, dendritic cell-based vaccines, nanoparticle delivery systems, and next-generation adjuvants. The review intends to make the case for personalized and antigen-specific vaccination strategies as a compelling option for precision immunotherapy primarily in oncology, where neoantigen-based vaccines are being developed. In addition, we will also review tolerogenic vaccination strategies, vaccination strategies targeting pathological proteins and pathways in neurodegenerative and cardiovascular diseases, and vaccine-based treatment of chronic viral infections. Current evidence about vaccines suggests that several ways are available to induce an immune response or create tolerance to a disease, and possibly altering its course instead of simply controlling the symptoms. However, many challenges are still to be overcome, including disease variability, how to identify appropriate target antigens, the complexity of manufacturing process, long-term safety, and integration with already established treatments. By virtue of the convergence of multiple fields—immunology, genomics, bioinformatics, and new delivery systems—precision vaccinology is gaining momentum. Thus, it is envisaged that personalized vaccines will be an increasingly essential component of future preventive and therapeutic approaches for non-communicable diseases.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Non-communicable diseases (NCDs), including cancer, cardiovascular, neurodegenerative, autoimmune, and allergic diseases, account for a significant amount of global morbidity and mortality.
Evidence base and method
Chronic viral infections have not been recognized as NCDs, despite the availability of several therapeutic vaccination strategies against oncogenic viruses, such as the hepatitis B virus (HBV) and the human papillomavirus (HPV).
Consensus and conclusions
Indeed, chronic viral infection leads to the development and progression of malignancies directly linked to the viruses.
Controversies and gaps
However, many challenges are still to be overcome, including disease variability, how to identify appropriate target antigens, the complexity of manufacturing process, long-term safety, and integration with already established treatments.
Research and practice implications
Recent advances in technology have paved the way to the use of vaccines beyond the prevention of infectious diseases, heralding innovative therapeutic and preventative strategies for a variety of chronic NCDs.

HPV 自我采样和分子生物标志物对 宫颈癌 筛查和分诊的临床影响:“时代正在改变”——全面回顾和未来展望

Clinical Impact of HPV Self-Sampling and Molecular Biomarkers on Cervical Cancer Screening and Triage: “The Times They Are A-Changin’”—A Comprehensive Review and Future Perspectives
作者： Carlo Antonio Liverani, Veronica Boero, Ermelinda Monti, Carlotta Caia, Leonardo Natalini, Paolo Vercellini, Michele Vignali, Andrea Ciavattini
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 (CC) 的必要但不充分的原因,反映了由免疫控制、病毒持久性和潜伏期形成的长期 宿主 病毒平衡。从基于细胞学的筛查到初级 高危型HPV 检测的转变极大地改善了宫颈癌前病变的早期发现,但也带来了与检测结果波动、过度诊断、过度治疗和患者焦虑相关的新的临床挑战,其程度可能因国家而异,具体取决于筛查组织和预防服务的获取情况。这篇叙述性综述在 HPV 自然史、持久性和免疫相互作用的知识不断发展的背景下,对基于 HPV 的 CC 筛查进行了临床导向的概述,并讨论了对检测结果和风险分层解释的影响。新出现的筛查和分诊方法,包括分子生物标志物、基于外泌体的生物标志物和人工智能支持的风险评估,正在重塑 CC 预防策略,能够更准确地识别面临临床重大疾病风险的女性。这些工具可能与基于自我采样的筛查途径特别相关,其中分子分类策略可以减少对临床医生收集额外样本的需求,并改善对 HPV 阳性女性的管理。与此同时,对病毒潜伏期和 宿主 相关进展决定因素的更好理解支持从二元测试解释向纵向和个体化风险评估的转变。将生物学洞察力与技术创新相结合可能有助于个性化筛查策略,保持高灵敏度,同时减少不必要的干预。HPV 相关疾病管理的进化信息和风险适应方法对于优化预防结果和保持基于人群的筛查计划的益处至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 HPV 自我采样和分子生物标志物对 宫颈癌 筛查和分诊的临床影响,重点关注 HPV 感染的自然史、其持续性、免疫相互作用以及对风险分层和癌前病变早期检测的影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合,没有明确提及系统搜索方法、数据库或荟萃分析。它讨论了从基于细胞学的筛查到初级 HPV 检测的转变、这种转变带来的挑战以及新筛查和分诊方法的潜力。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,转向基于 HPV 的筛查改善了宫颈癌前病变的早期检测,但也带来了检测结果波动、过度诊断和过度治疗等挑战。由于 HPV 感染的复杂性及其与 宿主 免疫系统的相互作用,它强调需要进行纵向和个体化的风险评估。
争议与缺口
关于 HPV 检测结果的解释存在争议,特别是在结果波动和过度诊断风险的背景下。该综述强调需要更好地了解影响疾病进展的病毒潜伏期和 宿主 相关因素,以及整合分子生物标志物和人工智能以进行更精确的风险评估。
科研与实践启示
该评论表明,将生物学见解与分子生物标志物和人工智能等技术创新相结合,可以改善个性化筛查策略。它还强调了制定风险适应方法来管理 HPV 相关疾病的重要性,以优化预防结果并保持基于人群的筛查计划的有效性。

Clinical Impact of HPV Self-Sampling and Molecular Biomarkers on Cervical Cancer Screening and Triage: “The Times They Are A-Changin’”—A Comprehensive Review and Future Perspectives

Authors: Carlo Antonio Liverani, Veronica Boero, Ermelinda Monti, Carlotta Caia, Leonardo Natalini, Paolo Vercellini, Michele Vignali, Andrea Ciavattini
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a necessary but insufficient cause of cervical cancer (CC) and reflects a long-standing host–virus equilibrium shaped by immune control, viral persistence, and latency. The transition from cytology-based screening to primary high-risk HPV testing has substantially improved early detection of cervical precancerous lesions but has also introduced new clinical challenges related to fluctuating test results, overdiagnosis, overtreatment, and patient anxiety, whose magnitude may vary across countries depending on screening organization and access to prevention services. This narrative review provides a clinically oriented overview of HPV-based CC screening in the context of evolving knowledge on HPV natural history, persistence, and immune interaction, and discusses the implications for the interpretation of test results and risk stratification. New emerging screening and triage approaches, including molecular biomarkers, exosome-based biomarkers, and artificial-intelligence-supported risk assessment, are reshaping CC prevention strategies by enabling more precise identification of women at risk for clinically significant disease. These tools may be particularly relevant in self-sampling-based screening pathways, where molecular triage strategies can reduce the need for additional clinician-collected samples and improve management of HPV-positive women. At the same time, improved understanding of viral latency and host-related determinants of progression supports a shift from binary test interpretation toward longitudinal and individualized risk assessment. Integrating biological insight with technological innovation may facilitate personalized screening strategies that maintain high sensitivity while reducing unnecessary interventions. An evolutionary-informed and risk-adapted approach to HPV-related disease management is essential to optimize prevention outcomes and preserve the benefits of population-based screening programs.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the clinical impact of HPV self-sampling and molecular biomarkers on cervical cancer screening and triage, with an emphasis on the natural history of HPV infection, its persistence, immune interactions, and the implications for risk stratification and early detection of precancerous lesions.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with no explicit mention of systematic search methods, databases, or meta-analysis. It discusses the transition from cytology-based screening to primary HPV testing, the challenges introduced by this shift, and the potential of new screening and triage approaches.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the shift to HPV-based screening has improved early detection of cervical precancerous lesions but has also introduced challenges such as fluctuating test results, overdiagnosis, and overtreatment. It emphasizes the need for longitudinal and individualized risk assessment due to the complexity of HPV infection and its interaction with the host immune system.
Controversies and gaps
There are controversies regarding the interpretation of HPV test results, particularly in the context of fluctuating outcomes and the risk of overdiagnosis. The review highlights the need for better understanding of viral latency and host-related factors that influence disease progression, as well as the integration of molecular biomarkers and AI for more precise risk assessment.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that integrating biological insights with technological innovations, such as molecular biomarkers and AI, can improve personalized screening strategies. It also highlights the importance of developing risk-adapted approaches to HPV-related disease management to optimize prevention outcomes and maintain the effectiveness of population-based screening programs.
